`apps/web/src/lib/map/route.test.ts`:

```ts
import { osakaTrip } from '@test/fixtures/trip';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { readTrip, type DayView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { createGoogleDirectionsUrl, createGoogleMapsUrl, getDayMapPoints, getDayRouteSegments, getPlaybackZoom, pointAlongPath } from './route';

const day = {
  number: 1,
  stops: [
    { key: 'a', title: 'Start', time: '09:00', transportIcon: '🚆', coordinates: [35, 135], approximate: false },
    { key: 'missing', title: 'No position', time: '09:30', approximate: false },
    {
      key: 'b',
      title: 'Finish',
      time: '10:00',
      transportIcon: '🚆',
      coordinates: [35.1, 135.2],
      approximate: true,
      transit: { mode: 'train', label: 'JR', icon: '🚆', waypoints: [[35.05, 135.1]] },
    },
  ],
} as unknown as DayView;

describe('route helpers ported from the Vite app', () => {
  it('turns located stops into ordered map points', () => {
    expect(getDayMapPoints(day)).toEqual([
      { key: 'a', title: 'Start', time: '09:00', coordinates: [35, 135], approximate: false },
      { key: 'b', title: 'Finish', time: '10:00', coordinates: [35.1, 135.2], approximate: true },
    ]);
  });

  it('creates a Google Maps search link without an API key', () => {
    expect(createGoogleMapsUrl([35.0175, 135.6784])).toBe('https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.0175%2C135.6784');
  });

  it('builds a segment through its waypoints in itinerary order', () => {
    expect(getDayRouteSegments(day)).toEqual([
      { fromKey: 'a', toKey: 'b', mode: 'train', label: 'JR', icon: '🚆', coordinates: [[35, 135], [35.05, 135.1], [35.1, 135.2]] },
    ]);
  });

  it('interpolates across every leg of a path', () => {
    const path: [number, number][] = [[0, 0], [0, 2], [2, 2]];
    expect(pointAlongPath(path, 0)).toEqual([0, 0]);
    expect(pointAlongPath(path, 0.5)).toEqual([0, 2]);
    expect(pointAlongPath(path, 1)).toEqual([2, 2]);
    expect(pointAlongPath([], 0.5)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('follows walking closer than buses and flights', () => {
    expect(getPlaybackZoom('walk')).toBeGreaterThan(getPlaybackZoom('bus'));
    expect(getPlaybackZoom('bus')).toBeGreaterThan(getPlaybackZoom('flight'));
    expect(getPlaybackZoom('unknown')).toBe(14);
  });

  it('keeps the real transport modes of the family itinerary', () => {
    const days = readTrip(osakaTrip()).days;
    const modes = (index: number) => new Map(getDayRouteSegments(days[index]!).map((s) => [s.toKey, s.mode]));

    expect(modes(0).get('d1-kix')).toBe('flight');
    expect(modes(0).get('d1-hotel')).toBe('bus');
    expect(modes(0).get('d1-castle')).toBe('train');
    expect(modes(2).get('d3-amanohashidate')).toBe('bus');
    expect(modes(2).get('d3-arrive')).toBe('bus');
    expect(modes(3).get('d4-kix')).toBe('train');
  });

  it('links Google directions with official parameters only', () => {
    const train = new URL(createGoogleDirectionsUrl({ mode: 'train', label: 'JR', coordinates: [[35, 135], [35.1, 135.2], [35.2, 135.3]] }));
    expect(`${train.origin}${train.pathname}`).toBe('https://www.google.com/maps/dir/');
    expect(train.searchParams.get('origin')).toBe('35,135');
    expect(train.searchParams.get('destination')).toBe('35.2,135.3');
    expect(train.searchParams.get('travelmode')).toBe('transit');
    expect(train.searchParams.has('waypoints')).toBe(false);

    const walk = new URL(createGoogleDirectionsUrl({ mode: 'walk', label: '도보', coordinates: [[35, 135], [35.1, 135.2], [35.2, 135.3]] }));
    expect(walk.searchParams.get('travelmode')).toBe('walking');
    expect(walk.searchParams.get('waypoints')).toBe('35.1,135.2');

    const tourBus = new URL(createGoogleDirectionsUrl({ mode: 'bus', label: '투어 버스', coordinates: [[35, 135], [35.2, 135.3]] }));
    expect(tourBus.searchParams.get('travelmode')).toBe('driving');
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/content.ts`:

```ts
import type { components } from '@/lib/api/schema';

type Schemas = components['schemas'];
export type TripDocument = Schemas['TripDocument'];
export type DayDocument = Schemas['DayDocument'];
export type StopDocument = Schemas['StopDocument'];
export type PlaceDocument = Schemas['PlaceDocument'];
export type TripSummary = Schemas['TripSummary'];
export type WeatherResponse = Schemas['WeatherResponse'];
export type MeResponse = Schemas['MeResponse'];

export type LatLng = [number, number];
export type LinkItem = { label: string; url: string; note?: string };
export type Photo = {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  credit?: string;
  license?: string;
  licenseUrl?: string;
  sourceUrl?: string;
  context?: string;
  focus?: string;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
};
export type Transit = { mode: string; label: string; icon?: string; waypoints: LatLng[] };
export type Fact = { label: string; value: string };
export type Block =
  | { type: 'paragraph'; title?: string; text: string }
  | { type: 'steps' | 'tips'; title?: string; items: string[] }
  | { type: 'facts'; title?: string; items: Fact[] }
  | { type: 'links'; title?: string; items: LinkItem[] }
  | { type: 'image'; title?: string; photo: Photo }
  | { type: 'html'; title?: string; html: string };
export type Guide = { kind: string; title: string; blocks: Block[] };
export type Fee = { title: string; adult?: string; child?: string; source?: LinkItem };
export type Tour = {
  title: string;
  provider?: string;
  checkedAt?: string;
  source?: LinkItem;
  meeting?: string;
  meetingMap?: string;
  departure?: string;
  returnTime?: string;
  included?: string;
  excluded?: string;
  feeNote?: string;
  preparation: string[];
  fees: Fee[];
};
export type ChecklistItem = { id: string; priority?: string; title: string; description?: string };
export type ChecklistGroup = { id: string; title: string; hint?: string; items: ChecklistItem[] };
export type LinkGroup = { label: string; links: LinkItem[] };
export type Flight = { direction: string; airline?: string; from: string; to: string; date: string; depart: string; arrive: string; note?: string };
export type Traveler = { label: string; emoji?: string };
export type RoutePoint = { code: string; label: string; vehicle?: string; vehicleLabel?: string };
export type LegacySection = { id: string; title: string; blocks: Block[] };
export type Provenance = { kind: string; createdAt?: string; method?: string; disclaimer?: string; referencedProducts: LinkItem[] };
export type TripContent = {
  coverPhoto?: Photo;
  travelers: Traveler[];
  flights: Flight[];
  route: RoutePoint[];
  linkGroups: LinkGroup[];
  checklistGroups: ChecklistGroup[];
  sections: LegacySection[];
  provenance?: Provenance;
};
export type PlaceInfo = {
  key: string;
  kind: string;
  name: string;
  area: string;
  branch?: string;
  category?: string;
  items?: string;
  menu?: string;
  address?: string;
  hours?: string;
  coordinates?: LatLng;
  mapUrl?: string;
  note?: string;
  checkedAt?: string;
  facts: Fact[];
  sources: LinkItem[];
  alternatives: string[];
  media: LinkItem[];
};
export type StopView = {
  id: string;
  key: string;
  time: string;
  title: string;
  type: string;
  details: string;
  duration?: string;
  buffer?: string;
  timing?: string;
  transportIcon?: string;
  coordinates?: LatLng;
  approximate: boolean;
  optional: boolean;
  transit?: Transit;
  photo?: Photo;
  hotelPhoto?: Photo;
  links: LinkItem[];
  guide?: Guide;
  placeKey?: string;
};
export type DayView = { id: string; number: number; date: string; city: string; theme: string; note?: string; tour?: Tour; stops: StopView[] };
export type TripView = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  timezone: string;
  visibility: string;
  isSample: boolean;
  permissions: { canEdit: boolean; canClone: boolean };
  content: TripContent;
  days: DayView[];
  places: PlaceInfo[];
};

const isRecord = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
const text = (v: unknown): string | undefined => (typeof v === 'string' && v.trim() !== '' ? v : undefined);
const list = (v: unknown): unknown[] => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []);
const texts = (v: unknown): string[] => list(v).map(text).filter((s): s is string => s !== undefined);
const num = (v: unknown): number | undefined => (typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : undefined);
function defined<T>(items: (T | undefined)[]): T[] {
  return items.filter((item): item is T => item !== undefined);
}

export function safeHref(url: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (typeof url !== 'string') return undefined;
  const value = url.trim();
  if (value.startsWith('#')) return value;
  if (value.startsWith('/') && !value.startsWith('//')) return value;
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(value);
    return ['http:', 'https:', 'mailto:', 'tel:'].includes(parsed.protocol) ? value : undefined;
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}

/** 이미지는 같은 사이트 경로나 https만 받는다. */
const safeImageSrc = (src: unknown) => {
  const href = safeHref(src);
  return href && (href.startsWith('/') || href.startsWith('https://')) ? href : undefined;
};

export function readLink(v: unknown): LinkItem | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(v)) return undefined;
  const label = text(v.label);
  const url = safeHref(v.url);
  if (!label || !url) return undefined;
  const note = text(v.note);
  return note ? { label, url, note } : { label, url };
}

const readLinks = (v: unknown) => defined(list(v).map(readLink));

export function readPhoto(v: unknown): Photo | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(v)) return undefined;
  const src = safeImageSrc(v.src);
  const alt = text(v.alt);
  if (!src || !alt) return undefined;
  const photo: Photo = { src, alt };
  for (const key of ['credit', 'license', 'context', 'focus'] as const) {
    const value = text(v[key]);
    if (value) photo[key] = value;
  }
  const licenseUrl = safeHref(v.licenseUrl);
  if (licenseUrl) photo.licenseUrl = licenseUrl;
  const sourceUrl = safeHref(v.sourceUrl ?? v.source);
  if (sourceUrl) photo.sourceUrl = sourceUrl;
  const width = num(v.width);
  const height = num(v.height);
  if (width && height) {
    photo.width = width;
    photo.height = height;
  }
  return photo;
}

export function readCoordinates(v: unknown): LatLng | undefined {
  if (!Array.isArray(v) || v.length !== 2) return undefined;
  const [lat, lng] = v;
  if (typeof lat !== 'number' || typeof lng !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng)) return undefined;
  if (lat < -90 || lat > 90 || lng < -180 || lng > 180) return undefined;
  return [lat, lng];
}

function readTransit(v: unknown): Transit | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(v)) return undefined;
  const mode = text(v.mode);
  const label = text(v.label);
  if (!mode || !label) return undefined;
  const icon = text(v.icon);
  const waypoints = defined(list(v.waypoints).map(readCoordinates));
  return icon ? { mode, label, icon, waypoints } : { mode, label, waypoints };
}

function readFact(v: unknown): Fact | undefined {
  if (Array.isArray(v)) {
    const [label, value] = v;
    return text(label) && text(value) ? { label: label as string, value: value as string } : undefined;
  }
  if (isRecord(v) && text(v.label) && text(v.value)) return { label: v.label as string, value: v.value as string };
  return undefined;
}

export function readBlock(v: unknown): Block | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(v)) return undefined;
  const title = text(v.title);
  const withTitle = <T extends object>(block: T) => (title ? { ...block, title } : block);
  switch (v.type) {
    case 'paragraph': {
      const value = text(v.text);
      return value ? withTitle({ type: 'paragraph' as const, text: value }) : undefined;
    }
    case 'steps':
    case 'tips': {
      const items = texts(v.items);
      return items.length ? withTitle({ type: v.type, items }) : undefined;
    }
    case 'facts': {
      const items = defined(list(v.items).map(readFact));
      return items.length ? withTitle({ type: 'facts' as const, items }) : undefined;
    }
    case 'links': {
      const items = readLinks(v.items);
      return items.length ? withTitle({ type: 'links' as const, items }) : undefined;
    }
    case 'image':
    case 'figure': {
      const photo = readPhoto(v.photo ?? v);
      return photo ? withTitle({ type: 'image' as const, photo }) : undefined;
    }
    case 'html': {
      const html = text(v.html);
      return html ? withTitle({ type: 'html' as const, html }) : undefined;
    }
    default:
      return undefined;
  }
}

const readBlocks = (v: unknown) => defined(list(v).map(readBlock));

function readGuide(v: unknown): Guide | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(v)) return undefined;
  const title = text(v.title);
  const blocks = readBlocks(v.blocks);
  if (!title || !blocks.length) return undefined;
  return { kind: text(v.kind) ?? 'place', title, blocks };
}

function readTour(v: unknown): Tour | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(v)) return undefined;
  const title = text(v.title);
  if (!title) return undefined;
  const fees = defined(
    list(v.fees).map((fee): Fee | undefined => {
      if (!isRecord(fee) || !text(fee.title)) return undefined;
      return { title: fee.title as string, adult: text(fee.adult), child: text(fee.child), source: readLink(fee.source) };
    }),
  );
  return {
    title,
    provider: text(v.provider),
    checkedAt: text(v.checkedAt),
    source: readLink(v.source),
    meeting: text(v.meeting),
    meetingMap: safeHref(v.meetingMap),
    departure: text(v.departure),
    returnTime: text(v.returnTime),
    included: text(v.included),
    excluded: text(v.excluded),
    feeNote: text(v.feeNote),
    preparation: texts(v.preparation),
    fees,
  };
}

function readContent(v: unknown): TripContent {
  const c = isRecord(v) ? v : {};
  const provenance = isRecord(c.provenance) && text(c.provenance.kind)
    ? {
        kind: c.provenance.kind as string,
        createdAt: text(c.provenance.createdAt),
        method: text(c.provenance.method),
        disclaimer: text(c.provenance.disclaimer),
        referencedProducts: readLinks(c.provenance.referencedProducts),
      }
    : undefined;
  return {
    coverPhoto: readPhoto(c.coverPhoto),
    travelers: defined(list(c.travelers).map((t) => (isRecord(t) && text(t.label) ? { label: t.label as string, emoji: text(t.emoji) } : undefined))),
    flights: defined(
      list(c.flights).map((f): Flight | undefined => {
        if (!isRecord(f)) return undefined;
        const [direction, from, to, date, depart, arrive] = [f.direction, f.from, f.to, f.date, f.depart, f.arrive].map(text);
        if (!direction || !from || !to || !date || !depart || !arrive) return undefined;
        return { direction, airline: text(f.airline), from, to, date, depart, arrive, note: text(f.note) };
      }),
    ),
    route: defined(
      list(c.route).map((r) =>
        isRecord(r) && text(r.code) && text(r.label)
          ? { code: r.code as string, label: r.label as string, vehicle: text(r.vehicle), vehicleLabel: text(r.vehicleLabel) }
          : undefined,
      ),
    ),
    linkGroups: defined(
      list(c.linkGroups).map((g) => {
        if (!isRecord(g) || !text(g.label)) return undefined;
        const links = readLinks(g.links);
        return links.length ? { label: g.label as string, links } : undefined;
      }),
    ),
    checklistGroups: defined(
      list(c.checklistGroups).map((g): ChecklistGroup | undefined => {
        if (!isRecord(g) || !text(g.id) || !text(g.title)) return undefined;
        const items = defined(
          list(g.items).map((i): ChecklistItem | undefined =>
            isRecord(i) && text(i.id) && text(i.title)
              ? { id: i.id as string, title: i.title as string, priority: text(i.priority), description: text(i.description) }
              : undefined,
          ),
        );
        return { id: g.id as string, title: g.title as string, hint: text(g.hint), items };
      }),
    ),
    sections: defined(
      list(c.sections).map((s) => {
        if (!isRecord(s) || !text(s.id) || !text(s.title)) return undefined;
        return { id: s.id as string, title: s.title as string, blocks: readBlocks(s.blocks) };
      }),
    ),
    provenance,
  };
}

function readPlace(p: PlaceDocument): PlaceInfo {
  const d = isRecord(p.details) ? p.details : {};
  return {
    key: p.key,
    kind: p.kind,
    name: p.name,
    area: p.area,
    branch: text(d.branch),
    category: text(d.category),
    items: text(d.items),
    menu: text(d.menu),
    address: text(d.address),
    hours: text(d.hours),
    coordinates: readCoordinates(d.coordinates),
    mapUrl: safeHref(d.mapUrl),
    note: text(d.note),
    checkedAt: text(d.checkedAt),
    facts: defined(list(d.facts).map(readFact)),
    sources: readLinks(d.sources),
    alternatives: texts(d.alternatives),
    media: readLinks(d.media),
  };
}

function readStop(s: StopDocument): StopView {
  return {
    id: s.id,
    key: s.key,
    time: s.time,
    title: s.title,
    type: s.type,
    details: s.details,
    duration: text(s.duration),
    buffer: text(s.buffer),
    timing: text(s.timing),
    transportIcon: text(s.transportIcon),
    coordinates: readCoordinates(s.coordinates),
    approximate: s.approximate,
    optional: s.optional,
    transit: readTransit(s.transit),
    photo: readPhoto(s.photo),
    hotelPhoto: readPhoto(s.hotelPhoto),
    links: readLinks(s.links),
    guide: readGuide(s.guide),
    placeKey: text(s.placeKey),
  };
}

export function readTrip(doc: TripDocument): TripView {
  return {
    id: doc.id,
    slug: doc.slug,
    title: doc.title,
    subtitle: text(doc.subtitle),
    startDate: doc.startDate,
    endDate: doc.endDate,
    timezone: doc.timezone,
    visibility: doc.visibility,
    isSample: doc.isSample,
    permissions: doc.permissions,
    content: readContent(doc.content),
    days: doc.days.map((day) => ({
      id: day.id,
      number: day.number,
      date: day.date,
      city: day.city,
      theme: day.theme,
      note: text(day.note),
      tour: readTour(day.tour),
      stops: day.stops.map(readStop),
    })),
    places: doc.places.map(readPlace),
  };
}

export const readSummaryCover = (summary: TripSummary) => readPhoto(summary.coverPhoto);
```

`readTrip`의 `stops.filter((s) => s.guide).length` 기대값 27은 샘플의 가이드 수(공항 1, 교통 7, 호텔 4, 장소 12, 조식 3)다. 숫자가 다르면 샘플을 다시 세고 테스트를 고치지 말고 해석기가 버린 가이드가 있는지 먼저 확인한다.

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/format.ts`:

```ts
import type { IconName } from '@/components/ui/icons.generated';
import type { StopView } from './content';

const WEEKDAYS = ['일', '월', '화', '수', '목', '금', '토'];
const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const parts = (iso: string) => iso.split('-').map(Number) as [number, number, number];
const utc = (iso: string) => {
  const [y, m, d] = parts(iso);
  return Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d);
};

export function formatDayDate(iso: string): string {
  const [, m, d] = parts(iso);
  return `${pad(m)}.${pad(d)} ${WEEKDAYS[new Date(utc(iso)).getUTCDay()]}`;
}

export function formatDateRange(start: string, end: string): string {
  const [sy, sm, sd] = parts(start);
  const [ey, em, ed] = parts(end);
  const head = `${sy}.${pad(sm)}.${pad(sd)}`;
  if (start === end) return head;
  return sy === ey ? `${head} – ${pad(em)}.${pad(ed)}` : `${head} – ${ey}.${pad(em)}.${pad(ed)}`;
}

/** 'N박'은 밤 비행이 끼면 날짜만으로 알 수 없다(다낭 샘플은 3박 5일). 날짜로 확실한 'N일'만 쓴다. */
export function tripLength(start: string, end: string): string {
  const days = Math.round((utc(end) - utc(start)) / 86_400_000) + 1;
  return days <= 1 ? '당일' : `${days}일`;
}

const TYPE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  airport: '공항',
  flight: '항공',
  transport: '이동',
  hotel: '숙소',
  meal: '식사',
  breakfast: '조식',
  sight: '관광',
  tour: '투어',
  shopping: '쇼핑',
  bath: '온천',
  free: '자유',
};

export const stopTypeLabel = (type: string) => TYPE_LABELS[type] ?? '일정';

const TRANSIT_ICONS: Record<string, IconName> = {
  flight: 'type-flight',
  train: 'type-train',
  cablecar: 'type-train',
  bus: 'type-bus',
  walk: 'type-walk',
  boat: 'type-boat',
  taxi: 'type-taxi',
  car: 'type-taxi',
};

export const transitIcon = (mode: string): IconName => TRANSIT_ICONS[mode] ?? 'route';

const TYPE_ICONS: Record<string, IconName> = {
  airport: 'type-airport',
  flight: 'type-flight',
  hotel: 'type-hotel',
  meal: 'type-meal',
  breakfast: 'type-meal',
  sight: 'type-sight',
  tour: 'type-sight',
  shopping: 'type-shopping',
  bath: 'type-free',
  free: 'type-free',
};

export function stopIcon(stop: Pick<StopView, 'type' | 'transit'>): IconName {
  if (stop.type === 'transport') return stop.transit ? transitIcon(stop.transit.mode) : 'route';
  return TYPE_ICONS[stop.type] ?? 'map-pin';
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/day.ts`:

```ts
export function todayIn(timezone: string, now: Date = new Date()): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: timezone, year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(now);
}

/** ?day=N이 맞으면 그 날, 아니면 여행 중인 오늘, 아니면 첫날. */
export function initialDayIndex(days: { date: string }[], param: string | string[] | undefined, today: string): number {
  const raw = Array.isArray(param) ? param[0] : param;
  const n = Number(raw);
  if (raw !== undefined && Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 1 && n <= days.length) return n - 1;
  const index = days.findIndex((day) => day.date === today);
  return index >= 0 ? index : 0;
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/completion.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';

type Snapshot = { stops: string[]; items: string[] };
const EMPTY: Snapshot = Object.freeze({ stops: [], items: [] }) as Snapshot;
const PREFIX = 'tripnote:completion:v1:';

const strings = (v: unknown) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v.filter((s): s is string => typeof s === 'string') : []);

export function createCompletionStore(slug: string, storage: Storage | undefined = globalThis.localStorage) {
  const key = `${PREFIX}${slug}`;
  const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
  let snapshot: Snapshot = load();

  function load(): Snapshot {
    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(storage?.getItem(key) ?? 'null');
      return { stops: strings(saved?.stops), items: strings(saved?.items) };
    } catch {
      return { stops: [], items: [] };
    }
  }

  function commit(next: Snapshot) {
    snapshot = next;
    try {
      storage?.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(next));
    } catch {
      // 사생활 보호 모드나 저장 공간 부족이면 이번 방문 동안만 기억한다.
    }
    for (const listener of listeners) listener();
  }

  const toggle = (values: string[], value: string) =>
    values.includes(value) ? values.filter((v) => v !== value) : [...values, value];

  return {
    getSnapshot: () => snapshot,
    subscribe(listener: () => void) {
      listeners.add(listener);
      const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {
        if (event.key === key) {
          snapshot = load();
          listener();
        }
      };
      globalThis.addEventListener?.('storage', onStorage);
      return () => {
        listeners.delete(listener);
        globalThis.removeEventListener?.('storage', onStorage);
      };
    },
    toggleStop: (stopKey: string) => commit({ ...snapshot, stops: toggle(snapshot.stops, stopKey) }),
    toggleItem: (itemId: string) => commit({ ...snapshot, items: toggle(snapshot.items, itemId) }),
  };
}

const stores = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof createCompletionStore>>();

/** 테스트마다 localStorage를 비우므로, 메모리에 남은 저장소도 같이 비운다. */
export function resetCompletionStores() {
  stores.clear();
}

function storeFor(slug: string) {
  let store = stores.get(slug);
  if (!store) {
    store = createCompletionStore(slug);
    stores.set(slug, store);
  }
  return store;
}

export function useCompletion(slug: string) {
  const store = storeFor(slug);
  const snapshot = useSyncExternalStore(store.subscribe, store.getSnapshot, () => EMPTY);
  return useMemo(
    () => ({
      stops: new Set(snapshot.stops) as ReadonlySet<string>,
      items: new Set(snapshot.items) as ReadonlySet<string>,
      toggleStop: store.toggleStop,
      toggleItem: store.toggleItem,
    }),
    [snapshot, store],
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/map/geo.ts`:

```ts
import type { LatLng } from '@/lib/trip/content';

/** 샘플과 API 좌표는 [위도, 경도]다. MapLibre와 GeoJSON은 [경도, 위도]다. 순서는 여기서만 바꾼다. */
export const toLngLat = ([lat, lng]: LatLng): [number, number] => [lng, lat];

export function boundsOf(points: LatLng[]): [[number, number], [number, number]] | undefined {
  if (!points.length) return undefined;
  const lats = points.map((p) => p[0]);
  const lngs = points.map((p) => p[1]);
  return [
    [Math.min(...lngs), Math.min(...lats)],
    [Math.max(...lngs), Math.max(...lats)],
  ];
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/map/route.ts`:

```ts
import type { DayView, LatLng, StopView } from '@/lib/trip/content';

export type MapPoint = { key: string; title: string; time: string; coordinates: LatLng; approximate: boolean };
export type RouteSegment = { fromKey: string; toKey: string; mode: string; label: string; icon: string; coordinates: LatLng[] };

const TRANSIT_DEFAULTS: Record<string, { label: string; icon: string }> = {
  train: { label: '전철', icon: '🚆' },
  bus: { label: '버스', icon: '🚌' },
  walk: { label: '도보', icon: '🚶' },
  flight: { label: '비행기', icon: '✈️' },
  boat: { label: '배', icon: '⛴️' },
  cablecar: { label: '케이블카', icon: '🚠' },
  taxi: { label: '택시', icon: '🚕' },
  car: { label: '차량', icon: '🚐' },
};

const PLAYBACK_ZOOMS: Record<string, number> = { walk: 16, bus: 14, taxi: 14, car: 14, train: 13, boat: 13, cablecar: 14, flight: 7 };

export const getPlaybackZoom = (mode: string) => PLAYBACK_ZOOMS[mode] ?? 14;

type Located = StopView & { coordinates: LatLng };
const located = (day: DayView) => day.stops.filter((s): s is Located => Array.isArray(s.coordinates));

export function getDayMapPoints(day: DayView): MapPoint[] {
  return located(day).map((s) => ({ key: s.key, title: s.title, time: s.time, coordinates: s.coordinates, approximate: Boolean(s.approximate) }));
}

/** 기존 앱처럼 transit이 없으면 이동 아이콘 이모지로 수단을 짐작한다. */
function inferMode(transportIcon = ''): string {
  if (transportIcon.includes('✈')) return 'flight';
  if (transportIcon.includes('🚌')) return 'bus';
  if (transportIcon.includes('🚆') || transportIcon.includes('🚇')) return 'train';
  if (transportIcon.includes('⛴') || transportIcon.includes('🚤')) return 'boat';
  if (transportIcon.includes('🚠')) return 'cablecar';
  if (transportIcon.includes('🚕')) return 'taxi';
  return 'walk';
}

export function getDayRouteSegments(day: DayView): RouteSegment[] {
  const stops = located(day);
  return stops.slice(1).map((stop, index) => {
    const previous = stops[index] as Located;
    const mode = stop.transit?.mode ?? inferMode(stop.transportIcon);
    const defaults = TRANSIT_DEFAULTS[mode];
    return {
      fromKey: previous.key,
      toKey: stop.key,
      mode,
      label: stop.transit?.label ?? defaults?.label ?? '이동',
      icon: stop.transit?.icon ?? defaults?.icon ?? '📍',
      coordinates: [previous.coordinates, ...(stop.transit?.waypoints ?? []), stop.coordinates],
    };
  });
}

export function pointAlongPath(path: LatLng[], progress: number): LatLng | null {
  if (!path.length) return null;
  if (path.length === 1) return path[0] as LatLng;
  const legs = path.slice(1).map((point, index) => {
    const previous = path[index] as LatLng;
    return Math.hypot(point[0] - previous[0], point[1] - previous[1]);
  });
  const total = legs.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d, 0);
  if (total === 0) return path.at(-1) as LatLng;
  let remaining = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress)) * total;
  for (let index = 0; index < legs.length; index += 1) {
    const leg = legs[index] as number;
    if (remaining <= leg || index === legs.length - 1) {
      const ratio = leg === 0 ? 1 : remaining / leg;
      const start = path[index] as LatLng;
      const end = path[index + 1] as LatLng;
      return [start[0] + (end[0] - start[0]) * ratio, start[1] + (end[1] - start[1]) * ratio];
    }
    remaining -= leg;
  }
  return path.at(-1) as LatLng;
}

export const createGoogleMapsUrl = (p: LatLng) =>
  `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(p.join(','))}`;

export function createGoogleDirectionsUrl(segment: Pick<RouteSegment, 'mode' | 'label' | 'coordinates'>): string {
  const points = segment.coordinates;
  const query = new URLSearchParams({ api: '1', origin: (points[0] as LatLng).join(','), destination: (points.at(-1) as LatLng).join(',') });
  const tourBus = segment.mode === 'bus' && /투어|전용/.test(segment.label);
  const mode =
    segment.mode === 'walk'
      ? 'walking'
      : segment.mode === 'train' || (segment.mode === 'bus' && !tourBus)
        ? 'transit'
        : segment.mode === 'bus' || segment.mode === 'taxi' || segment.mode === 'car'
          ? 'driving'
          : null;
  if (mode) query.set('travelmode', mode);
  if (mode && mode !== 'transit' && points.length > 2) {
    query.set('waypoints', points.slice(1, -1).slice(0, 3).map((p) => p.join(',')).join('|'));
  }
  return `https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?${query.toString()}`;
}
```

### Task 6: 가이드 블록 렌더러와 이관 HTML 정리

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/html/sanitize.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/trip/ExternalLink.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/trip/PhotoFigure.tsx`, `PhotoFigure.module.css`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/trip/GuideBlocks.tsx`, `GuideBlocks.module.css`
- Test: `apps/web/src/lib/html/sanitize.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/trip/GuideBlocks.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Block`, `Photo`, `LinkItem`, `readTrip`(Task 5), `osakaTrip`(픽스처).
- Produces:
  - `sanitizeLegacyHtml(html: string): string`(`@/lib/html/sanitize`). 허용 태그는 문단, 제목(h2~h5), 목록, 정의 목록, 표, `figure`, `img`, `a`, `details`, `summary`, 강조 태그뿐이다. 속성은 `a[href|title]`, `img[src|alt|width|height]`만 남기고 `class`, `id`, `style`, 이벤트 속성은 모두 지운다. 링크 스킴은 `http`, `https`, `mailto`, `tel`과 상대 경로, 이미지는 `https`와 사이트 경로만. 외부 링크에는 `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`를 붙인다. 이미지에는 `loading="lazy" decoding="async"`를 붙인다.
  - `<ExternalLink href children className? />`: `http(s)`면 새 창과 화면 낭독용 "(새 창)", 그 밖에는 같은 창.
  - `<PhotoFigure photo: Photo variant?: 'wide'|'hotel' />`: 사진, 맥락, 출처와 라이선스 캡션.
  - `<GuideBlocks blocks: Block[] headingLevel?: 3|4 />`: 블록 종류별 렌더러. `html` 블록은 `sanitizeLegacyHtml`을 거쳐 `.legacy` 산문 스타일 안에 넣는다.

- [ ] **Step 1: 의존성**

Run: `cd apps/web && pnpm add sanitize-html@2.18.1 && pnpm add -D @types/sanitize-html`

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`apps/web/src/lib/html/sanitize.test.ts`:

```ts
import { osakaTrip } from '@test/fixtures/trip';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { readTrip } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { sanitizeLegacyHtml } from './sanitize';

describe('sanitizeLegacyHtml', () => {
  it('removes scripts, styles, frames and event handlers', () => {
    const dirty =
      '<p onclick="steal()">안내</p><script>alert(1)</script><style>body{display:none}</style>' +
      '<iframe src="https://evil.example"></iframe><img src="/images/a.jpg" alt="사진" onerror="steal()">';
    const clean = sanitizeLegacyHtml(dirty);

    expect(clean).not.toMatch(/script|style|iframe|onclick|onerror|steal/);
    expect(clean).toContain('<p>안내</p>');
    expect(clean).toContain('<img src="/images/a.jpg" alt="사진" loading="lazy" decoding="async" />');
  });

  it('drops javascript and protocol-relative urls', () => {
    const clean = sanitizeLegacyHtml(
      '<a href="javascript:alert(1)">나쁜 링크</a><a href="//evil.example/x">상대 프로토콜</a><img src="//evil.example/p.png" alt="x"><img src="http://plain.example/p.png" alt="y">',
    );

    expect(clean).not.toContain('javascript:');
    expect(clean).not.toContain('evil.example');
    expect(clean).not.toContain('plain.example');
    expect(clean).toContain('나쁜 링크');
  });

  it('opens external links in a new tab and keeps in-page anchors in place', () => {
    const clean = sanitizeLegacyHtml('<a href="https://www.osakacastle.net/">공식</a><a href="#preparation" data-view="preparation">준비물</a>');

    expect(clean).toContain('<a href="https://www.osakacastle.net/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">공식</a>');
    expect(clean).toContain('<a href="#preparation">준비물</a>');
  });

  it('strips classes, ids and inline styles so legacy markup cannot borrow app styles', () => {
    const clean = sanitizeLegacyHtml('<div class="skip-link" id="main" style="position:fixed"><b>여권</b></div>');
    expect(clean).toBe('<div><b>여권</b></div>');
  });

  it('keeps the wording of every legacy block in the Osaka Kyoto original', () => {
    const trip = readTrip(osakaTrip());
    const blocks = [...trip.days.flatMap((d) => d.stops.flatMap((s) => s.guide?.blocks ?? [])), ...trip.content.sections.flatMap((s) => s.blocks)];
    const html = blocks.filter((b) => b.type === 'html');

    expect(html.length).toBe(25);
    for (const block of html) {
      const clean = sanitizeLegacyHtml(block.html);
      expect(clean).not.toMatch(/<script|on\w+=|javascript:/i);
      const visible = (s: string) =>
        s
          .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')
          .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
          .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
          .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
          .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
          .replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
          .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
          .trim();
      expect(visible(clean)).toBe(visible(block.html));
    }
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Block } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { GuideBlocks } from './GuideBlocks';

const blocks: Block[] = [
  { type: 'paragraph', text: '공원 안에서는 아래 지도 번호를 따라가요.' },
  { type: 'steps', title: '가는 길', items: ['히가시우메다역으로 걸어가요.', '다니마치선을 타요.'] },
  { type: 'facts', items: [{ label: '출발', value: '우메다 점심 후' }] },
  { type: 'tips', items: ['물을 챙겨요.'] },
  { type: 'links', items: [{ label: '오사카성 · 교통', url: 'https://www.osakacastle.net/access/' }] },
  { type: 'image', photo: { src: '/images/places/osaka-castle.webp', alt: '오사카성 천수각', credit: 'DXR', license: 'CC BY-SA 4.0', licenseUrl: 'https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0' } },
  { type: 'html', title: '면세품 수령', html: '<h3>출국 전 면세품 수령</h3><p><b>여권 원본</b>을 준비해요.</p><script>alert(1)</script>' },
];

describe('GuideBlocks', () => {
  it('renders each block type with its own semantics', () => {
    const { container } = render(<GuideBlocks blocks={blocks} />);

    expect(screen.getByText('공원 안에서는 아래 지도 번호를 따라가요.').tagName).toBe('P');
    const steps = screen.getByRole('heading', { name: '가는 길' }).nextElementSibling as HTMLElement;
    expect(within(steps).getAllByRole('listitem')).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(steps.tagName).toBe('OL');
    expect(container.querySelector('dt')?.textContent).toBe('출발');
    expect(container.querySelector('dd')?.textContent).toBe('우메다 점심 후');
    expect(screen.getByText('물을 챙겨요.').closest('ul')).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('opens outside links in a new tab and says so to screen readers', () => {
    render(<GuideBlocks blocks={blocks} />);
    const link = screen.getByRole('link', { name: /오사카성 · 교통/ });

    expect(link).toHaveAttribute('target', '_blank');
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute('rel', 'noopener noreferrer');
    expect(link).toHaveTextContent('(새 창)');
  });

  it('credits photos and sanitizes legacy html', () => {
    const { container } = render(<GuideBlocks blocks={blocks} />);

    expect(screen.getByRole('img', { name: '오사카성 천수각' })).toHaveAttribute('loading', 'lazy');
    expect(screen.getByText(/사진: DXR/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: /CC BY-SA 4.0/ })).toHaveAttribute('href', 'https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0');
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: '출국 전 면세품 수령' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(container.innerHTML).not.toContain('<script');
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/html/sanitize.ts`:

```ts
import sanitizeHtml from 'sanitize-html';

const isExternal = (href: string | undefined) => !!href && /^https?:\/\//i.test(href);

// transformTags가 더하는 target, rel, loading, decoding도 허용 목록을 거치므로 함께 적는다.
const OPTIONS: sanitizeHtml.IOptions = {
  allowedTags: [
    'section', 'aside', 'div', 'p', 'h2', 'h3', 'h4', 'h5', 'b', 'strong', 'em', 'i', 'small', 'mark', 'span', 'br', 'hr',
    'ul', 'ol', 'li', 'dl', 'dt', 'dd', 'a', 'img', 'figure', 'figcaption', 'table', 'thead', 'tbody', 'tr', 'th', 'td',
    'details', 'summary', 'blockquote', 'code', 'sup', 'sub',
  ],
  allowedAttributes: {
    a: ['href', 'title', 'target', 'rel'],
    img: ['src', 'alt', 'width', 'height', 'loading', 'decoding'],
    td: ['colspan', 'rowspan'],
    th: ['colspan', 'rowspan', 'scope'],
  },
  allowedSchemes: ['http', 'https', 'mailto', 'tel'],
  allowedSchemesByTag: { img: ['https'] },
  allowProtocolRelative: false,
  transformTags: {
    a: (tagName, attribs) => ({
      tagName,
      attribs: isExternal(attribs.href) ? { ...attribs, target: '_blank', rel: 'noopener noreferrer' } : attribs,
    }),
    img: (tagName, attribs) => ({ tagName, attribs: { ...attribs, loading: 'lazy', decoding: 'async' } }),
  },
  // http 이미지나 프로토콜 상대 주소는 src가 지워진다. src 없는 빈 이미지는 통째로 지운다.
  exclusiveFilter: (frame) => frame.tag === 'img' && !frame.attribs.src,
};

/** 이관 데이터의 HTML만 받는다. 허용 목록 밖의 태그와 속성, 스크립트 스킴은 모두 지운다. */
export function sanitizeLegacyHtml(html: string): string {
  return sanitizeHtml(html, OPTIONS);
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/ExternalLink.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

type Props = { href: string; children: ReactNode; className?: string };

export function ExternalLink({ href, children, className }: Props) {
  if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(href)) {
    return (
      <a href={href} className={className}>
        {children}
      </a>
    );
  }
  return (
    <a href={href} className={className} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
      {children}
      <span className="visually-hidden"> (새 창)</span>
    </a>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Photo } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { ExternalLink } from './ExternalLink';
import styles from './PhotoFigure.module.css';

type Props = { photo: Photo; variant?: 'wide' | 'hotel' };

export function PhotoFigure({ photo, variant = 'wide' }: Props) {
  const classes = [styles.figure, variant === 'hotel' ? styles.hotel : '', photo.focus === 'top' ? styles.top : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  return (
    <figure className={classes}>
      {/* biome-ignore lint/performance/noImgElement: 샘플 이미지는 5번 계획에서 저장소로 옮긴 뒤 next/image로 바꾼다 */}
      <img src={photo.src} alt={photo.alt} width={photo.width ?? 1200} height={photo.height ?? 675} loading="lazy" decoding="async" />
      {photo.credit || photo.context ? (
        <figcaption className={styles.caption}>
          {photo.context ? (
            <>
              {photo.context}
              <br />
            </>
          ) : null}
          {photo.credit ? <>사진: {photo.credit}</> : null}
          {photo.sourceUrl ? (
            <>
              {' · '}
              <ExternalLink href={photo.sourceUrl}>{variant === 'hotel' ? '호텔 공식 안내' : '원본'}</ExternalLink>
            </>
          ) : null}
          {photo.license && photo.licenseUrl ? (
            <>
              {' · '}
              <ExternalLink href={photo.licenseUrl}>{photo.license}</ExternalLink>
            </>
          ) : null}
        </figcaption>
      ) : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/GuideBlocks.tsx`:

```tsx
import { sanitizeLegacyHtml } from '@/lib/html/sanitize';
import type { Block } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { ExternalLink } from './ExternalLink';
import styles from './GuideBlocks.module.css';
import { PhotoFigure } from './PhotoFigure';

type Props = { blocks: Block[]; headingLevel?: 3 | 4 };

export function GuideBlocks({ blocks, headingLevel = 4 }: Props) {
  const Heading = headingLevel === 3 ? 'h3' : 'h4';
  return (
    <div className={styles.blocks}>
      {blocks.map((block, index) => (
        // 블록에는 id가 없다. 같은 가이드 안에서 순서가 바뀌지 않으므로 위치를 키로 쓴다.
        // biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noArrayIndexKey: 블록 순서는 고정이다
        <section key={index}>
          {block.title && block.type !== 'html' ? <Heading className={styles.heading}>{block.title}</Heading> : null}
          <BlockBody block={block} />
        </section>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function BlockBody({ block }: { block: Block }) {
  switch (block.type) {
    case 'paragraph':
      return <p>{block.text}</p>;
    case 'steps':
      return (
        <ol className={styles.steps}>
          {block.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item}>{item}</li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      );
    case 'tips':
      return (
        <ul className={styles.tips}>
          {block.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item}>{item}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      );
    case 'facts':
      return (
        <dl className={styles.facts}>
          {block.items.map((fact) => (
            <div key={fact.label}>
              <dt>{fact.label}</dt>
              <dd>{fact.value}</dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
      );
    case 'links':
      return (
        <ul className={styles.links}>
          {block.items.map((link) => (
            <li key={link.url}>
              <ExternalLink href={link.url}>{link.label}</ExternalLink>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      );
    case 'image':
      return <PhotoFigure photo={block.photo} />;
    case 'html':
      return (
        <div
          className={styles.legacy}
          // 이관 데이터의 HTML은 허용 목록으로 정리한 뒤에만 넣는다.
          // biome-ignore lint/security/noDangerouslySetInnerHtml: sanitizeLegacyHtml을 거친 값
          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: sanitizeLegacyHtml(block.html) }}
        />
      );
  }
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createServerApi`, `serverApi`(Task 3), `readTrip`, `TripView`, `DayView`, `StopView`, `formatDayDate`, `formatDateRange`, `tripLength`, `stopTypeLabel`, `stopIcon`, `initialDayIndex`, `todayIn`, `useCompletion`, `createGoogleMapsUrl`(Task 5), `GuideBlocks`, `PhotoFigure`, `ExternalLink`(Task 6), UI 컴포넌트(Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `fetchTrip(api: Client<paths>, slug: string): Promise<TripLoad>`와 `loadTrip(slug): Promise<TripLoad>`(`@/lib/trip/load`, React `cache`로 한 요청 안에서 한 번만 부름). `TripLoad = { kind: 'ok'; trip: TripDocument } | { kind: 'missing' } | { kind: 'unavailable' }`.
  - `<TripScreen trip: TripView initialDay: number />`(클라이언트). 이후 과제가 이 컴포넌트의 표시된 자리에 지도(Task 9), 섹션 내비와 섹션(Task 8), 날씨(Task 10)를 끼운다.
  - `<DayView day trip mode onModeChange done onToggleStop hideDone onHideDoneChange onLocate? resources? />`, `<StopRow stop index done onToggle mode onLocate? />`, `<DayBar days activeIndex onSelect />`, `<TripHeader trip />`.
  - 스톱 행 요소에 `id="stop-<key>"`를 단다. 지도와 "명소 가는 길"이 이 id로 스크롤한다.

화면 규칙(설계 2.1, 2.2, 디자인 시스템 StopRow와 DayBar):
- 상단은 로고 링크와 여행 제목(`display-lg`), 기간(`data`), 동행 이모지와 라벨. 편집 권한이 없으면 `보기 전용` 배지와 "복제해서 내 일정으로 만들기는 곧 열려요."를 보인다(복제 버튼은 5번 계획). 조사 예시(`provenance.kind === 'researched-example'`)는 "여러 여행 상품을 참고해 새로 짠 예시 일정이에요. 운영 정보는 방문 전 공식 안내를 확인해 주세요."를 덧붙인다.
- 날짜 바는 sticky. 각 칸은 `N일차`, `10.21 수`, 도시. 선택한 칸은 `aria-current="date"`. 바꾸면 주소가 `?day=N`(해시 유지)으로 바뀐다.
- 날짜 머리는 `N일차 · 10.21 수`, 테마 문장(`display-md`), 완료 수 `3 / 12 완료`.
- 보기 방식 `간략 일정`/`상세 일정`. 상세면 모든 스톱의 상세가 열린다. `완료한 일정 숨기기` 토글.
- 스톱 행: 시각(`data-lg`)과 근거 수준(`timing`), 유형 아이콘과 라벨, 선택 일정이면 `선택` 배지, 제목, 소요시간, 위치 버튼(좌표가 있을 때), 버퍼 안내(경고 톤), 상세 안내(사진, 호텔 사진, 본문, Google 지도와 출처 링크, 가이드 디스클로저), 완료 버튼(44px 원, `aria-pressed`, 라벨 `일정 완료 표시: <제목>`).

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/load.test.ts`:

```ts
import { osakaTrip } from '@test/fixtures/trip';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createServerApi } from '@/lib/api/server-client';
import { fetchTrip } from './load';

const api = (respond: () => Response | Promise<Response>) => {
  const fetch = vi.fn(async () => respond());
  return { fetch, client: createServerApi('', { baseUrl: 'http://api.test', fetch }) };
};

describe('fetchTrip', () => {
  it('returns the document on 200', async () => {
    const { client } = api(() => Response.json(osakaTrip()));
    const result = await fetchTrip(client, 'osaka-kyoto-family-2026');
    expect(result.kind).toBe('ok');
  });

  it('treats 404 as missing, which also covers private trips', async () => {
    const { client } = api(() => Response.json({ detail: '일정을 찾을 수 없어요.' }, { status: 404 }));
    expect(await fetchTrip(client, 'someone-elses-trip')).toEqual({ kind: 'missing' });
  });

  it('treats server errors and network failures as unavailable', async () => {
    expect(await fetchTrip(api(() => new Response('boom', { status: 500 })).client, 'a')).toEqual({ kind: 'unavailable' });
    expect(await fetchTrip(api(() => Promise.reject(new TypeError('fetch failed'))).client, 'a')).toEqual({ kind: 'unavailable' });
  });

  it('does not call the api for a slug that cannot exist', async () => {
    const { client, fetch } = api(() => Response.json({}));
    expect(await fetchTrip(client, '../../me')).toEqual({ kind: 'missing' });
    expect(await fetchTrip(client, 'a'.repeat(101))).toEqual({ kind: 'missing' });
    expect(fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/TripScreen.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { danangTrip, osakaTrip } from '@test/fixtures/trip';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { readTrip } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { TripScreen } from './TripScreen';

const osaka = readTrip(osakaTrip());
const day1 = osaka.days[0]!;
const day2 = osaka.days[1]!;

describe('TripScreen', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/t/osaka-kyoto-family-2026');
  });

  it('shows the trip header with dates, length and a read-only badge', () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);

    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: osaka.title })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('2026.10.21 – 10.24 · 4일')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('보기 전용')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const travelers = within(screen.getByRole('list', { name: '함께 가는 사람' })).getAllByRole('listitem');
    expect(travelers.map((li) => li.textContent)).toEqual(['👩 엄마', '👨 아빠', '👦 아들']);
  });

  it('marks a researched sample as an example', () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={readTrip(danangTrip())} initialDay={0} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('샘플 일정')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/여러 여행 상품을 참고해 새로 짠 예시 일정이에요/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('lists every stop of the selected day in order', () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    const list = screen.getByRole('list', { name: '1일차 일정' });

    // 이관 HTML 가이드 안에도 h3가 있으므로 행마다 첫 h3만 읽는다.
    const titles = [...list.querySelectorAll(':scope > li')].map((li) => li.querySelector('h3')?.textContent);
    expect(titles).toEqual(day1.stops.map((s) => s.title));
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: day1.theme })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('switches days from the day bar and keeps the choice in the address', async () => {
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/t/osaka-kyoto-family-2026#itinerary');
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    const bar = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: '날짜 선택' });

    await userEvent.click(within(bar).getByRole('button', { name: /2일차/ }));

    expect(within(bar).getByRole('button', { name: /2일차/ })).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'date');
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: day2.theme })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(window.location.search).toBe('?day=2');
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#itinerary');
  });

  it('opens every stop detail in detail mode', async () => {
    const { container } = render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('details[data-stop-detail][open]')).toHaveLength(0);

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '상세 일정' }));

    expect(container.querySelectorAll('details[data-stop-detail][open]')).toHaveLength(day1.stops.length);
  });

  it('checks a stop, counts it, remembers it and can hide it', async () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    const first = day1.stops[0]!;

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `일정 완료 표시: ${first.title}` }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `일정 완료 표시: ${first.title}` })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByText(`1 / ${day1.stops.length} 완료`)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tripnote:completion:v1:osaka-kyoto-family-2026') ?? '{}').stops).toEqual([first.key]);

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '완료한 일정 숨기기' }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('heading', { level: 3, name: first.title })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('links a located stop to Google Maps and shows its details', async () => {
    const { container } = render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    const kix = day1.stops.find((s) => s.key === 'd1-kix')!;
    const row = container.querySelector('#stop-d1-kix') as HTMLElement;

    await act(async () => {
      row.querySelector('summary')?.click();
    });

    expect(row.querySelector('details')).toHaveAttribute('open');
    expect(within(row).getByRole('link', { name: /Google 지도/ })).toHaveAttribute('href', 'https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.4347%2C135.244');
    expect(within(row).getByText(kix.details)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('calls onLocate when the location button is pressed', async () => {
    const onLocate = vi.fn();
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} onLocate={onLocate} />);
    const kix = day1.stops.find((s) => s.key === 'd1-kix')!;

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `${kix.title} 지도에서 보기` }));

    expect(onLocate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('d1-kix');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 불러오기 구현**

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/load.ts`:

```ts
import { cache } from 'react';
import type { Client } from 'openapi-fetch';
import type { paths } from '@/lib/api/schema';
import type { TripDocument } from './content';

export type TripLoad = { kind: 'ok'; trip: TripDocument } | { kind: 'missing' } | { kind: 'unavailable' };

const SLUG = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,100}$/;

export async function fetchTrip(api: Client<paths>, slug: string): Promise<TripLoad> {
  if (!SLUG.test(slug)) return { kind: 'missing' };
  try {
    const { data, response } = await api.GET('/api/v1/trips/{slug}', { params: { path: { slug } } });
    if (data) return { kind: 'ok', trip: data };
    return response.status === 404 ? { kind: 'missing' } : { kind: 'unavailable' };
  } catch {
    return { kind: 'unavailable' };
  }
}

/** generateMetadata와 페이지가 같은 요청 안에서 한 번만 부르게 한다. */
export const loadTrip = cache(async (slug: string) => {
  const { serverApi } = await import('@/lib/api/server');
  return fetchTrip(await serverApi(), slug);
});
```

`loadTrip`은 `server-only`가 걸린 모듈을 동적으로 불러 `fetchTrip` 테스트가 `server-only`를 import하지 않게 한다.

`apps/web/src/components/trip/TripHeader.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui';
import type { TripView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { formatDateRange, tripLength } from '@/lib/trip/format';
import styles from './TripHeader.module.css';

export function TripHeader({ trip }: { trip: TripView }) {
  const readOnly = !trip.permissions.canEdit;
  const researched = trip.content.provenance?.kind === 'researched-example';
  return (
    <header className={styles.header}>
      <div className={styles.inner}>
        <Link href="/" className={styles.brand} aria-label="여행노트 홈">
          {/* biome-ignore lint/performance/noImgElement: 로고 SVG */}
          <img src="/brand/lockup-horizontal.svg" alt="" width={120} height={28} />
        </Link>
        <h1 className={styles.title}>{trip.title}</h1>
        {trip.subtitle ? <p className={styles.subtitle}>{trip.subtitle}</p> : null}
        <span className={styles.dates}>
          {formatDateRange(trip.startDate, trip.endDate)} · {tripLength(trip.startDate, trip.endDate)}
        </span>
        {trip.content.travelers.length ? (
          <ul className={styles.travelers} aria-label="함께 가는 사람">
            {trip.content.travelers.map((t) => (
              <li key={t.label}>
                {t.emoji ? <span aria-hidden="true">{t.emoji} </span> : null}
                {t.label}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : null}
        <div className={styles.notice}>
          {trip.isSample ? <Badge tone="brand">샘플 일정</Badge> : null}
          {readOnly ? <Badge>보기 전용</Badge> : null}
          {readOnly ? <p>복제해서 내 일정으로 만들기는 곧 열려요.</p> : null}
        </div>
        {researched ? (
          <p className={styles.notice}>여러 여행 상품을 참고해 새로 짠 예시 일정이에요. 운영 정보는 방문 전 공식 안내를 확인해 주세요.</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/DayBar.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { DayView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { formatDayDate } from '@/lib/trip/format';
import styles from './DayBar.module.css';

type Props = { days: DayView[]; activeIndex: number; onSelect: (index: number) => void };

export function DayBar({ days, activeIndex, onSelect }: Props) {
  return (
    <nav className={styles.bar} aria-label="날짜 선택">
      <ul className={styles.list}>
        {days.map((day, index) => (
          <li key={day.id}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.day}
              aria-current={index === activeIndex ? 'date' : undefined}
              onClick={() => onSelect(index)}
            >
              <span className={styles.number}>{day.number}일차</span>
              <span className={styles.date}>{formatDayDate(day.date)}</span>
              <span className={styles.city}>{day.city}</span>
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/StopRow.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Badge, Disclosure, Icon } from '@/components/ui';
import type { StopView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { stopIcon, stopTypeLabel } from '@/lib/trip/format';
import { createGoogleMapsUrl } from '@/lib/map/route';
import { ExternalLink } from './ExternalLink';
import { GuideBlocks } from './GuideBlocks';
import { PhotoFigure } from './PhotoFigure';
import styles from './StopRow.module.css';

type Props = {
  stop: StopView;
  done: boolean;
  mode: 'summary' | 'detail';
  onToggle: (key: string) => void;
  onLocate?: (key: string) => void;
};

export function StopRow({ stop, done, mode, onToggle, onLocate }: Props) {
  return (
    <li id={`stop-${stop.key}`} className={[styles.row, done ? styles.done : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ')}>
      <div className={styles.time}>
        {stop.time}
        {stop.timing ? <small className={styles.timing}>{stop.timing}</small> : null}
      </div>
      <div className={styles.main}>
        <span className={styles.type}>
          <Icon name={stopIcon(stop)} size={16} />
          {stopTypeLabel(stop.type)}
          {stop.optional ? <Badge tone="check">선택</Badge> : null}
        </span>
        <h3 className={styles.title}>{stop.title}</h3>
        <div className={styles.meta}>
          {stop.duration ? (
            <span>
              <Icon name="clock" size={16} />
              {stop.duration}
            </span>
          ) : null}
          {stop.coordinates && onLocate ? (
            <button type="button" className={styles.locate} onClick={() => onLocate(stop.key)} aria-label={`${stop.title} 지도에서 보기`}>
              <Icon name="map-pin" size={16} />
              위치
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        {stop.buffer ? (
          <p className={styles.buffer}>
            <Icon name="timer" size={16} />
            {stop.buffer}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {/* 보기 방식을 바꾸면 key가 바뀌어 모든 행의 열림 상태가 그 방식으로 다시 맞춰진다. */}
        <details key={mode} data-stop-detail className={styles.detail} open={mode === 'detail'}>
          <summary className={styles.locate}>
            상세 안내<span className="visually-hidden"> · {stop.title}</span>
            <Icon name="chevron-down" size={16} />
          </summary>
          <div className={styles.detailBody}>
            {stop.photo ? <PhotoFigure photo={stop.photo} /> : null}
            {stop.hotelPhoto ? <PhotoFigure photo={stop.hotelPhoto} variant="hotel" /> : null}
            <p className={styles.details}>{stop.details}</p>
            {stop.coordinates || stop.links.length ? (
              <div className={styles.sources}>
                {stop.coordinates ? <ExternalLink href={createGoogleMapsUrl(stop.coordinates)}>Google 지도</ExternalLink> : null}
                {stop.links.map((link) => (
                  <ExternalLink key={link.url} href={link.url}>
                    {link.label}
                  </ExternalLink>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : null}
            {stop.guide ? (
              <Disclosure summary={stop.guide.title} className={styles.guide} id={`guide-${stop.key}`}>
                <GuideBlocks blocks={stop.guide.blocks} />
              </Disclosure>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </details>
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.check}
        aria-pressed={done}
        aria-label={`일정 완료 표시: ${stop.title}`}
        onClick={() => onToggle(stop.key)}
      >
        <Icon name="check" />
      </button>
    </li>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/DayView.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Button, SegmentedControl } from '@/components/ui';
import type { DayView as Day } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { formatDayDate } from '@/lib/trip/format';
import styles from './DayView.module.css';
import { StopRow } from './StopRow';

type Props = {
  day: Day;
  mode: 'summary' | 'detail';
  onModeChange: (mode: 'summary' | 'detail') => void;
  done: ReadonlySet<string>;
  onToggleStop: (key: string) => void;
  hideDone: boolean;
  onHideDoneChange: (hide: boolean) => void;
  onLocate?: (key: string) => void;
  before?: ReactNode;
  resources?: ReactNode;
};

export function DayView({ day, mode, onModeChange, done, onToggleStop, hideDone, onHideDoneChange, onLocate, before, resources }: Props) {
  const completed = day.stops.filter((s) => done.has(s.key)).length;
  const visible = hideDone ? day.stops.filter((s) => !done.has(s.key)) : day.stops;
  return (
    <article className={styles.day} aria-labelledby={`day-${day.number}-theme`}>
      <header>
        <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
          {day.number}일차 · {formatDayDate(day.date)} · {day.city}
        </p>
        <h2 id={`day-${day.number}-theme`} className={styles.theme}>
          {day.theme}
        </h2>
        <p className={styles.score} aria-live="polite">
          {completed} / {day.stops.length} 완료
        </p>
      </header>
      {before}
      <div className={styles.toolbar}>
        <SegmentedControl
          label="일정 보기 방식"
          value={mode}
          onChange={onModeChange}
          options={[
            { value: 'summary', label: '간략 일정' },
            { value: 'detail', label: '상세 일정' },
          ]}
        />
        <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" icon="check" aria-pressed={hideDone} onClick={() => onHideDoneChange(!hideDone)}>
          완료한 일정 숨기기
        </Button>
      </div>
      <ol className={styles.stops} aria-label={`${day.number}일차 일정`}>
        {visible.map((stop) => (
          <StopRow key={stop.id} stop={stop} done={done.has(stop.key)} mode={mode} onToggle={onToggleStop} onLocate={onLocate} />
        ))}
      </ol>
      <div className={styles.resources}>
        {day.note ? <p className={styles.note}>{day.note}</p> : null}
        {resources}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/TripScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useCompletion } from '@/lib/trip/completion';
import type { TripView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { DayBar } from './DayBar';
import { DayView } from './DayView';
import { TripHeader } from './TripHeader';
import styles from './TripScreen.module.css';

type Props = { trip: TripView; initialDay: number; onLocate?: (key: string) => void };

export function TripScreen({ trip, initialDay, onLocate }: Props) {
  const [dayIndex, setDayIndex] = useState(initialDay);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'summary' | 'detail'>('summary');
  const [hideDone, setHideDone] = useState(false);
  const completion = useCompletion(trip.slug);
  const day = trip.days[dayIndex] ?? trip.days[0];

  function selectDay(index: number) {
    setDayIndex(index);
    const url = new URL(window.location.href);
    url.searchParams.set('day', String(index + 1));
    window.history.replaceState(window.history.state, '', `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`);
  }

  const locate =
    onLocate ??
    ((key: string) => {
      document.getElementById(`stop-${key}`)?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' });
    });

  if (!day) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <TripHeader trip={trip} />
      <DayBar days={trip.days} activeIndex={dayIndex} onSelect={selectDay} />
      <main id="main" className={styles.main}>
        {/* Task 8: 섹션 내비와 맛집, 쇼핑, 링크, 준비 섹션이 여기 들어온다. */}
        <section id="itinerary" aria-label="일정">
          <DayView
            day={day}
            mode={mode}
            onModeChange={setMode}
            done={completion.stops}
            onToggleStop={completion.toggleStop}
            hideDone={hideDone}
            onHideDoneChange={setHideDone}
            onLocate={locate}
            before={null /* Task 9: 그날 지도 */}
            resources={null /* Task 8: 투어 안내, 명소 가는 길. Task 10: 날씨 */}
          />
        </section>
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/t/[slug]/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { TripScreen } from '@/components/trip/TripScreen';
import { TripSkeleton } from '@/components/trip/TripSkeleton';
import { readTrip } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { initialDayIndex, todayIn } from '@/lib/trip/day';
import { loadTrip } from '@/lib/trip/load';

type Props = { params: Promise<{ slug: string }>; searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>> };

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: Props): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const result = await loadTrip(slug);
  if (result.kind !== 'ok') return { title: '일정을 찾을 수 없어요', robots: { index: false } };
  const trip = result.trip;
  return {
    title: trip.title,
    description: trip.subtitle ?? `${trip.days.length}일 여행 일정`,
    // 검색 엔진에는 public 여행만 보인다(설계 3.7).
    robots: { index: trip.visibility === 'public', follow: trip.visibility === 'public' },
  };
}

export default function TripPage(props: Props) {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<TripSkeleton />}>
      <TripContent {...props} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

async function TripContent({ params, searchParams }: Props) {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const query = await searchParams;
  const result = await loadTrip(slug);
  if (result.kind === 'missing') notFound();
  if (result.kind === 'unavailable') throw new Error('trip api unavailable');
  const trip = readTrip(result.trip);
  return <TripScreen trip={trip} initialDay={initialDayIndex(trip.days, query.day, todayIn(trip.timezone))} />;
}
```

### Task 8: 섹션 내비와 맛집, 쇼핑, 링크, 준비물, 투어 안내

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/trip/sections.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/trip/SectionNav.tsx`, `SectionNav.module.css`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/trip/PlaceCard.tsx`, `PlaceCard.module.css`, `PlacesSection.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/trip/LinksSection.tsx`, `ChecklistSection.tsx`, `Sections.module.css`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/trip/TourGuide.tsx`, `TourGuide.module.css`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/trip/SightGuideIndex.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/trip/TripInfo.tsx` (항공편, 이동 경로, 이관 안내 섹션)
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/trip/TripScreen.tsx`, `TripScreen.module.css`
- Test: `apps/web/src/lib/trip/sections.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/trip/TripSections.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TripView`, `PlaceInfo`, `LinkGroup`, `ChecklistGroup`, `Tour`, `LegacySection`, `useCompletion`(Task 5), `GuideBlocks`, `ExternalLink`(Task 6), `TripScreen`, `DayView`의 `resources` 자리(Task 7), UI 컴포넌트(Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `SECTIONS`, `type SectionId = 'itinerary'|'food'|'shopping'|'links'|'preparation'`, `sectionFromHash(hash): SectionId`, `useHashSection(): SectionId`, `legacySectionsFor(trip: TripView, section: SectionId | 'info'): LegacySection[]`(`@/lib/trip/sections`).
  - `<SectionNav active: SectionId />`(모바일은 하단 고정, 600px 이상은 상단 탭).
  - `<PlacesSection trip kind: 'dining'|'shopping' />`, `<LinksSection trip />`, `<ChecklistSection trip done onToggle />`, `<TourGuide tour date />`, `<SightGuideIndex day />`, `<TripInfo trip />`.

이관 섹션 규칙(오사카·교토의 `sections`): `food`, `shopping`, `links`, `preparation`은 같은 내용이 구조화 데이터(`places`, `linkGroups`, `checklistGroups`)로 이미 있으면 숨기고, 없을 때만 그 탭에 보인다. `overview`, `airport`처럼 탭이 없는 섹션은 일정 탭 아래 "여행 전체 안내"에 접힌 채로 보인다. 원본 문구를 잃지 않으면서 같은 내용이 두 번 나오지 않게 하는 규칙이다.

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/sections.test.ts`:

```ts
import { danangTrip, osakaTrip } from '@test/fixtures/trip';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { readTrip } from './content';
import { legacySectionsFor, sectionFromHash } from './sections';

describe('sectionFromHash', () => {
  it('maps known hashes and falls back to the itinerary', () => {
    expect(sectionFromHash('#food')).toBe('food');
    expect(sectionFromHash('#preparation')).toBe('preparation');
    expect(sectionFromHash('')).toBe('itinerary');
    expect(sectionFromHash('#stop-d1-kix')).toBe('itinerary');
  });
});

describe('legacySectionsFor', () => {
  const osaka = readTrip(osakaTrip());

  it('hides legacy tabs that structured data already covers', () => {
    expect(legacySectionsFor(osaka, 'food')).toEqual([]);
    expect(legacySectionsFor(osaka, 'shopping')).toEqual([]);
    expect(legacySectionsFor(osaka, 'links')).toEqual([]);
    expect(legacySectionsFor(osaka, 'preparation')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('keeps sections without a tab under trip info', () => {
    expect(legacySectionsFor(osaka, 'info').map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(['overview', 'airport']);
  });

  it('shows a legacy tab when the structured data for it is empty', () => {
    const doc = osakaTrip();
    doc.places = doc.places.filter((p) => p.kind !== 'dining');
    expect(legacySectionsFor(readTrip(doc), 'food').map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(['food']);
    expect(legacySectionsFor(readTrip(danangTrip()), 'info')).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/TripSections.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { danangTrip, osakaTrip } from '@test/fixtures/trip';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { readTrip } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { TripScreen } from './TripScreen';

const osaka = readTrip(osakaTrip());

async function goTo(hash: string) {
  await act(async () => {
    window.location.hash = hash;
    window.dispatchEvent(new HashChangeEvent('hashchange'));
  });
}

describe('trip sections', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/t/osaka-kyoto-family-2026');
  });

  it('offers the five sections and starts on the itinerary', () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    const nav = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: '여행 노트 섹션' });

    expect(within(nav).getAllByRole('link').map((a) => a.textContent)).toEqual(['일정', '맛집', '쇼핑', '링크', '준비']);
    expect(within(nav).getByRole('link', { name: '일정' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
    expect(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: '날짜 선택' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows dining and shopping candidates as place cards', async () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);

    await goTo('#food');
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '맛집 후보' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('article')).toHaveLength(osaka.places.filter((p) => p.kind === 'dining').length);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('navigation', { name: '날짜 선택' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await goTo('#shopping');
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '쇼핑 후보' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('article')).toHaveLength(osaka.places.filter((p) => p.kind === 'shopping').length);
  });

  it('groups booking and official links', async () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    await goTo('#links');

    const groups = screen.getAllByRole('heading', { level: 3 }).map((h) => h.textContent);
    expect(groups).toEqual(osaka.content.linkGroups.map((g) => g.label));
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('link', { name: /새 창/ }).length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
  });

  it('checks preparation items and remembers them', async () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    await goTo('#preparation');
    const first = osaka.content.checklistGroups[0]!.items[0]!;

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: new RegExp(first.title) }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: new RegExp(first.title) })).toBeChecked();
    expect(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tripnote:completion:v1:osaka-kyoto-family-2026') ?? '{}').items).toEqual([first.id]);
  });

  it('opens on the section named in the address', () => {
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/t/osaka-kyoto-family-2026#preparation');
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '준비물' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('keeps the legacy overview and airport guides under trip info, collapsed', () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    const info = screen.getByRole('region', { name: '여행 전체 안내' });

    const summaries = [...info.querySelectorAll(':scope > details > summary')].map((s) => s.textContent);
    expect(summaries).toEqual(['항공편과 이동 경로', '여행 개요', '공항 이용 안내']);
    expect(info.querySelectorAll(':scope > details[open]')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('shows the day tour with its fees on the tour day', () => {
    const tourDay = osaka.days.findIndex((d) => d.tour);
    render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={tourDay} />);

    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: '바다의 교토 일일투어' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('입장료·승선료 확인')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/뷰랜드 왕복 리프트/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('lists the day places with directions and opens a guide from the index', async () => {
    const { container } = render(<TripScreen trip={osaka} initialDay={0} />);
    const index = screen.getByRole('region', { name: '명소 가는 길' });
    const firstButton = within(index).getAllByRole('button')[0]!;
    const key = firstButton.getAttribute('data-stop-key')!;

    await userEvent.click(firstButton);

    expect(container.querySelector(`#guide-${key}`)).toHaveAttribute('open');
    expect(container.querySelector(`#stop-${key} details[data-stop-detail]`)).toHaveAttribute('open');
  });

  it('shows flights for a trip that has no legacy overview', () => {
    render(<TripScreen trip={readTrip(danangTrip())} initialDay={0} />);
    const info = screen.getByRole('region', { name: '여행 전체 안내' });
    expect(within(info).getByText('항공편과 이동 경로')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/sections.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import type { LegacySection, TripView } from './content';

export const SECTIONS = ['itinerary', 'food', 'shopping', 'links', 'preparation'] as const;
export type SectionId = (typeof SECTIONS)[number];

export function sectionFromHash(hash: string): SectionId {
  const id = hash.replace(/^#/, '');
  return (SECTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(id) ? (id as SectionId) : 'itinerary';
}

function subscribe(onChange: () => void) {
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', onChange);
  return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', onChange);
}

export function useHashSection(): SectionId {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, () => sectionFromHash(window.location.hash), () => 'itinerary');
}

const TABBED = new Set(['food', 'shopping', 'links', 'preparation']);

function covered(trip: TripView, id: string): boolean {
  switch (id) {
    case 'food':
      return trip.places.some((p) => p.kind === 'dining');
    case 'shopping':
      return trip.places.some((p) => p.kind === 'shopping');
    case 'links':
      return trip.content.linkGroups.length > 0;
    case 'preparation':
      return trip.content.checklistGroups.length > 0;
    default:
      return false;
  }
}

/** 이관 섹션 중 지금 보여 줄 것. 'info'는 탭이 없는 섹션(개요, 공항 안내 등)이다. */
export function legacySectionsFor(trip: TripView, section: SectionId | 'info'): LegacySection[] {
  return trip.content.sections.filter((s) => {
    if (section === 'info') return !TABBED.has(s.id);
    return s.id === section && !covered(trip, s.id);
  });
}
```

```tsx
import { Icon, type IconName } from '@/components/ui';
import type { SectionId } from '@/lib/trip/sections';
import styles from './SectionNav.module.css';

const ITEMS: { id: SectionId; label: string; icon: IconName }[] = [
  { id: 'itinerary', label: '일정', icon: 'nav-itinerary' },
  { id: 'food', label: '맛집', icon: 'nav-food' },
  { id: 'shopping', label: '쇼핑', icon: 'nav-shopping' },
  { id: 'links', label: '링크', icon: 'nav-links' },
  { id: 'preparation', label: '준비', icon: 'nav-prep' },
];

export function SectionNav({ active }: { active: SectionId }) {
  return (
    <nav className={styles.nav} aria-label="여행 노트 섹션">
      <ul className={styles.list}>
        {ITEMS.map((item) => (
          <li key={item.id}>
            <a href={`#${item.id}`} className={styles.item} aria-current={item.id === active ? 'page' : undefined}>
              <Icon name={item.icon} size={24} />
              {item.label}
            </a>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui';
import type { PlaceInfo } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { createGoogleMapsUrl } from '@/lib/map/route';
import { ExternalLink } from './ExternalLink';
import styles from './PlaceCard.module.css';

export function PlaceCard({ place }: { place: PlaceInfo }) {
  const rows: [string, string | undefined][] = [
    ['메뉴', place.menu],
    ['품목', place.items],
    ['영업', place.hours],
    ['주소', place.address],
  ];
  const mapUrl = place.mapUrl ?? (place.coordinates ? createGoogleMapsUrl(place.coordinates) : undefined);
  return (
    <article className={styles.card} aria-labelledby={`place-${place.key}`}>
      <div className={styles.head}>
        <div>
          <h3 id={`place-${place.key}`} className={styles.name}>
            {place.name}
          </h3>
          {place.branch ? <p className={styles.branch}>{place.branch}</p> : null}
        </div>
        <Badge>{place.area}</Badge>
      </div>
      {place.category ? <Badge tone="brand">{place.category}</Badge> : null}
      <dl className={styles.facts}>
        {[...rows, ...place.facts.map((f) => [f.label, f.value] as [string, string])]
          .filter((row): row is [string, string] => Boolean(row[1]))
          .map(([label, value]) => (
            <div key={`${label}-${value}`}>
              <dt>{label}</dt>
              <dd>{value}</dd>
            </div>
          ))}
      </dl>
      {place.note ? <p className={styles.note}>{place.note}</p> : null}
      {place.alternatives.length ? (
        <>
          <p className={styles.branch}>대안</p>
          <ul className={styles.alternatives}>
            {place.alternatives.map((a) => (
              <li key={a}>{a}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </>
      ) : null}
      <div className={styles.foot}>
        {mapUrl ? <ExternalLink href={mapUrl}>Google 지도</ExternalLink> : null}
        {[...place.sources, ...place.media].map((link) => (
          <ExternalLink key={link.url} href={link.url}>
            {link.label}
          </ExternalLink>
        ))}
        {place.checkedAt ? <span className={styles.checked}>{place.checkedAt} 확인</span> : null}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { EmptyState } from '@/components/ui';
import type { TripView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { legacySectionsFor } from '@/lib/trip/sections';
import { GuideBlocks } from './GuideBlocks';
import { PlaceCard } from './PlaceCard';
import styles from './Sections.module.css';

const COPY = {
  dining: { id: 'food', title: '맛집 후보', empty: '아직 맛집 후보가 없어요.' },
  shopping: { id: 'shopping', title: '쇼핑 후보', empty: '아직 쇼핑 후보가 없어요.' },
} as const;

export function PlacesSection({ trip, kind }: { trip: TripView; kind: 'dining' | 'shopping' }) {
  const copy = COPY[kind];
  const places = trip.places.filter((p) => p.kind === kind);
  const legacy = legacySectionsFor(trip, copy.id);
  return (
    <section id={copy.id} className={styles.section} aria-labelledby={`${copy.id}-title`}>
      <h2 id={`${copy.id}-title`} className={styles.title}>
        {copy.title}
      </h2>
      {places.length ? (
        <div className={styles.grid}>
          {places.map((place) => (
            <PlaceCard key={place.key} place={place} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {legacy.map((section) => (
        <GuideBlocks key={section.id} blocks={section.blocks} headingLevel={3} />
      ))}
      {!places.length && !legacy.length ? <EmptyState illustration="empty-search" title={copy.empty} headingLevel={3} /> : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```
