```tsx
import { Badge, EmptyState } from '@/components/ui';
import type { TripView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { legacySectionsFor } from '@/lib/trip/sections';
import { GuideBlocks } from './GuideBlocks';
import styles from './Sections.module.css';

type Props = { trip: TripView; done: ReadonlySet<string>; onToggle: (id: string) => void };

export function ChecklistSection({ trip, done, onToggle }: Props) {
  const groups = trip.content.checklistGroups;
  const legacy = legacySectionsFor(trip, 'preparation');
  return (
    <section id="preparation" className={styles.section} aria-labelledby="preparation-title">
      <h2 id="preparation-title" className={styles.title}>
        준비물
      </h2>
      <p className={styles.lead}>체크한 항목은 이 기기에만 저장돼요.</p>
      {groups.map((group) => (
        <div key={group.id} className={styles.group}>
          <h3 className={styles.groupTitle}>{group.title}</h3>
          {group.hint ? <p className={styles.lead}>{group.hint}</p> : null}
          <ul className={styles.checklist}>
            {group.items.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.id}>
                <label className={styles.check}>
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={done.has(item.id)} onChange={() => onToggle(item.id)} />
                  <span className={styles.checkText}>
                    <span className={styles.checkTitle}>
                      {item.title}
                      {item.priority ? <Badge tone={item.priority === '필수' ? 'check' : 'neutral'}>{item.priority}</Badge> : null}
                    </span>
                    {item.description ? <span className={styles.checkDesc}>{item.description}</span> : null}
                  </span>
                </label>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      ))}
      {legacy.map((section) => (
        <GuideBlocks key={section.id} blocks={section.blocks} headingLevel={3} />
      ))}
      {!groups.length && !legacy.length ? <EmptyState illustration="empty-checklist" title="아직 준비물 목록이 없어요." headingLevel={3} /> : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Disclosure } from '@/components/ui';
import type { Tour } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { formatDayDate } from '@/lib/trip/format';
import { ExternalLink } from './ExternalLink';
import styles from './TourGuide.module.css';

export function TourGuide({ tour, date }: { tour: Tour; date: string }) {
  return (
    <section className={styles.tour} aria-labelledby="tour-title">
      <span className={styles.provider}>
        {[tour.provider, formatDayDate(date)].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}
      </span>
      <h3 id="tour-title" className={styles.title}>
        {tour.title}
      </h3>
      {tour.source ? <ExternalLink href={tour.source.url}>{tour.source.label}</ExternalLink> : null}
      <dl className={styles.facts}>
        {tour.departure ? (
          <div>
            <dt>출발</dt>
            <dd>{tour.departure}</dd>
          </div>
        ) : null}
        {tour.returnTime ? (
          <div>
            <dt>귀환</dt>
            <dd>{tour.returnTime}</dd>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </dl>
      {tour.meeting ? (
        <p className={styles.meeting}>
          집합 {tour.meeting}
          {tour.meetingMap ? (
            <>
              {' · '}
              <ExternalLink href={tour.meetingMap}>위치 찾기</ExternalLink>
            </>
          ) : null}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <Disclosure summary="포함 사항과 준비할 것">
        <p>포함: {tour.included ?? '상품 안내 확인'}</p>
        <p>별도: {tour.excluded ?? '상품 안내 확인'}</p>
        {tour.preparation.length ? (
          <ul className={styles.list}>
            {tour.preparation.map((item) => (
              <li key={item}>{item}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : null}
      </Disclosure>
      {tour.fees.length || tour.feeNote ? (
        <Disclosure summary="입장료·승선료 확인">
          <div className={styles.fees}>
            {tour.fees.map((fee) => (
              <section key={fee.title}>
                <h4>{fee.title}</h4>
                <p>{[fee.adult, fee.child].filter(Boolean).join(' / ')}</p>
                {fee.source ? <ExternalLink href={fee.source.url}>{fee.source.label}</ExternalLink> : null}
              </section>
            ))}
            {tour.feeNote ? <p>{tour.feeNote}</p> : null}
          </div>
        </Disclosure>
      ) : null}
      {tour.checkedAt ? <p className={styles.checked}>{tour.checkedAt} 상품·운영사 안내 확인 · 시간은 교통 상황에 따라 달라져요.</p> : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/SightGuideIndex.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Button } from '@/components/ui';
import type { DayView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import styles from './Sections.module.css';

/** 장소와 코스 가이드가 있는 스톱만 모아, 누르면 그 스톱의 상세와 가이드를 열고 그 자리로 간다. */
export function SightGuideIndex({ day }: { day: DayView }) {
  const stops = day.stops.filter((s) => s.guide && ['place', 'course'].includes(s.guide.kind));
  if (!stops.length) return null;

  function open(key: string) {
    const row = document.getElementById(`stop-${key}`);
    const detail = row?.querySelector<HTMLDetailsElement>('details[data-stop-detail]');
    if (detail) detail.open = true;
    const guide = document.getElementById(`guide-${key}`) as HTMLDetailsElement | null;
    if (guide) guide.open = true;
    (guide ?? row)?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'start' });
  }

  return (
    <section className={styles.group} aria-label="명소 가는 길">
      <h3 className={styles.groupTitle}>명소 가는 길</h3>
      <p className={styles.lead}>방문할 곳을 고르면 이동 순서와 방문 팁으로 바로 가요.</p>
      <div className={styles.links}>
        {stops.map((stop) => (
          <Button key={stop.key} size="sm" variant="secondary" data-stop-key={stop.key} onClick={() => open(stop.key)}>
            {stop.title}
          </Button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/trip/TripInfo.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Disclosure } from '@/components/ui';
import type { TripView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { formatDayDate } from '@/lib/trip/format';
import { legacySectionsFor } from '@/lib/trip/sections';
import { GuideBlocks } from './GuideBlocks';
import styles from './Sections.module.css';

const DIRECTION: Record<string, string> = { outbound: '가는 편', return: '오는 편' };

export function TripInfo({ trip }: { trip: TripView }) {
  const legacy = legacySectionsFor(trip, 'info');
  const { flights, route } = trip.content;
  if (!legacy.length && !flights.length && !route.length) return null;
  return (
    <section className={styles.info} aria-label="여행 전체 안내">
      <h2 className={styles.title}>여행 전체 안내</h2>
      {flights.length || route.length ? (
        <Disclosure summary="항공편과 이동 경로">
          <ul className={styles.flights}>
            {flights.map((f) => (
              <li key={`${f.direction}-${f.date}`}>
                {DIRECTION[f.direction] ?? f.direction} · {formatDayDate(f.date)} · {f.airline ? `${f.airline} · ` : ''}
                <span className={styles.code}>
                  {f.from} {f.depart} → {f.to} {f.arrive}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          {route.length ? (
            <p className={styles.lead}>
              {route.map((r) => r.label).join(' → ')}
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </Disclosure>
      ) : null}
      {legacy.map((section) => (
        <Disclosure key={section.id} summary={section.title}>
          <GuideBlocks blocks={section.blocks} headingLevel={3} />
        </Disclosure>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: TripScreen에 섹션을 연결**

`apps/web/src/components/trip/TripScreen.tsx`를 다음으로 바꾼다(Task 7의 동작은 그대로, 섹션 전환과 자리 채우기만 더한다):

```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useCompletion } from '@/lib/trip/completion';
import type { TripView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { useHashSection } from '@/lib/trip/sections';
import { ChecklistSection } from './ChecklistSection';
import { DayBar } from './DayBar';
import { DayView } from './DayView';
import { LinksSection } from './LinksSection';
import { PlacesSection } from './PlacesSection';
import { SectionNav } from './SectionNav';
import { SightGuideIndex } from './SightGuideIndex';
import { TourGuide } from './TourGuide';
import { TripHeader } from './TripHeader';
import { TripInfo } from './TripInfo';
import styles from './TripScreen.module.css';

type Props = { trip: TripView; initialDay: number; onLocate?: (key: string) => void };

export function TripScreen({ trip, initialDay, onLocate }: Props) {
  const [dayIndex, setDayIndex] = useState(initialDay);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'summary' | 'detail'>('summary');
  const [hideDone, setHideDone] = useState(false);
  const completion = useCompletion(trip.slug);
  const section = useHashSection();
  const day = trip.days[dayIndex] ?? trip.days[0];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (section !== 'itinerary') document.getElementById('main')?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'start' });
  }, [section]);

  function selectDay(index: number) {
    setDayIndex(index);
    const url = new URL(window.location.href);
    url.searchParams.set('day', String(index + 1));
    window.history.replaceState(window.history.state, '', `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`);
  }

  const locate =
    onLocate ??
    ((key: string) => {
      document.getElementById(`stop-${key}`)?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'center' });
    });

  if (!day) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <TripHeader trip={trip} />
      <SectionNav active={section} />
      {section === 'itinerary' ? <DayBar days={trip.days} activeIndex={dayIndex} onSelect={selectDay} /> : null}
      <main id="main" className={styles.main}>
        {section === 'itinerary' ? (
          <section id="itinerary" aria-label="일정">
            <DayView
              day={day}
              mode={mode}
              onModeChange={setMode}
              done={completion.stops}
              onToggleStop={completion.toggleStop}
              hideDone={hideDone}
              onHideDoneChange={setHideDone}
              onLocate={locate}
              before={null /* Task 9: 그날 지도 */}
              resources={
                <>
                  {/* Task 10: 날씨 카드 */}
                  {day.tour ? <TourGuide tour={day.tour} date={day.date} /> : null}
                  <SightGuideIndex day={day} />
                </>
              }
            />
            <TripInfo trip={trip} />
          </section>
        ) : null}
        {section === 'food' ? <PlacesSection trip={trip} kind="dining" /> : null}
        {section === 'shopping' ? <PlacesSection trip={trip} kind="shopping" /> : null}
        {section === 'links' ? <LinksSection trip={trip} /> : null}
        {section === 'preparation' ? <ChecklistSection trip={trip} done={completion.items} onToggle={completion.toggleItem} /> : null}
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```

`TripScreen.module.css`의 `.main` 아래에 하단 내비 높이만큼 여백을 더한다:

```css
@media (max-width: 599px) {
  .main {
    padding-bottom: calc(var(--nav-height) + var(--space-8) + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
  }
}
```

---
### Task 9: MapLibre 하루 지도와 경로 재생

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/map/style.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/map/TripMap.tsx`, `TripMap.module.css`, `LazyTripMap.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/map/DayRoute.tsx`, `DayRoute.module.css`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/trip/TripScreen.tsx` (`before` 자리와 위치 버튼)
- Test: `apps/web/src/lib/map/style.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/map/DayRoute.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `MapPoint`, `RouteSegment`, `getDayMapPoints`, `getDayRouteSegments`, `pointAlongPath`, `getPlaybackZoom`, `createGoogleMapsUrl`, `createGoogleDirectionsUrl`(Task 5), `toLngLat`, `boundsOf`(Task 5), `colors`(Task 2), `Disclosure`, `Button`(Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `localizeLabels(style: StyleSpecification): StyleSpecification`(이름을 쓰는 심볼 레이어의 `text-field`를 `["coalesce", ["get","name:ko"], ["get","name"]]`로 바꿈), `transitColor(mode: string): ColorName`, `isDashed(mode: string): boolean`, `routeFeatures(segments: RouteSegment[], palette: Record<ColorName,string>): FeatureCollection<LineString>`(`@/lib/map/style`).
  - `<DayRoute day selectedKey? onSelect(key) />`: 지도, 이동 순서 목록, 교통 범례, 구간별 Google 길찾기, 안내 문구.
  - `<TripMap points segments selectedKey? onSelect />`(기본 내보내기, 클라이언트 전용). 지도 컨테이너의 `data-center="lng,lat"`에 현재 중심을 소수 넷째 자리까지 적는다. E2E가 좌표 순서를 확인하는 데 쓴다.
  - 환경 변수 `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAP_STYLE_URL`(기본 `https://tiles.openfreemap.org/styles/liberty`).

지도 규칙(디자인 시스템 모션, ADR 0007):
- 지도는 `next/dynamic`으로 클라이언트에서만 불러온다. 서버 HTML에는 자리 표시(`surface-sunken`, "지도를 불러오는 중이에요")만 있다.
- 핀은 MapLibre `Marker` 안의 `<button>`이다(번호, `aria-label="N번째 장소 <제목>"`). 키보드로 고를 수 있다.
- 경로선 색은 교통 토큰(항공 보라, 철도 파랑, 버스·택시·차량 갈색, 도보 초록 점선, 선박 청록). 항공도 점선이다.
- 경로 재생은 보이는 지도에서만 돈다. 화면 밖으로 나가면 멈춘다. `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`면 재생 버튼 대신 "움직임 줄이기 설정이 켜져 있어 경로 재생을 껐어요."를 보인다.
- 스톱 행의 `위치` 버튼을 누르면 그 핀으로 지도를 옮기고 지도를 화면 가운데로 스크롤한다.
- 한 손가락 끌기로 페이지 스크롤이 막히지 않게 `cooperativeGestures`를 켠다.

`apps/web/src/lib/map/style.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { StyleSpecification } from 'maplibre-gl';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { isDashed, localizeLabels, routeFeatures, transitColor } from './style';

const style = {
  version: 8,
  sources: {},
  layers: [
    { id: 'water', type: 'fill', source: 'x', 'source-layer': 'water' },
    { id: 'place', type: 'symbol', source: 'x', 'source-layer': 'place', layout: { 'text-field': '{name:latin}' } },
    { id: 'poi', type: 'symbol', source: 'x', 'source-layer': 'poi', layout: { 'text-field': ['coalesce', ['get', 'name_en'], ['get', 'name']] } },
    { id: 'shield', type: 'symbol', source: 'x', 'source-layer': 'road', layout: { 'text-field': '{ref}' } },
    { id: 'housenumber', type: 'symbol', source: 'x', 'source-layer': 'housenumber', layout: { 'text-field': '{housenumber}' } },
  ],
} as unknown as StyleSpecification;

describe('localizeLabels', () => {
  it('prefers Korean names on every layer that shows a name', () => {
    const localized = localizeLabels(style);
    const field = (id: string) => (localized.layers.find((l) => l.id === id) as { layout?: Record<string, unknown> }).layout?.['text-field'];

    expect(field('place')).toEqual(['coalesce', ['get', 'name:ko'], ['get', 'name']]);
    expect(field('poi')).toEqual(['coalesce', ['get', 'name:ko'], ['get', 'name']]);
    expect(field('shield')).toBe('{ref}');
    expect(field('housenumber')).toBe('{housenumber}');
  });

  it('does not change the style it was given', () => {
    localizeLabels(style);
    expect((style.layers[1] as { layout: Record<string, unknown> }).layout['text-field']).toBe('{name:latin}');
  });
});

describe('route styling', () => {
  it('uses transit tokens and dashes walking and flights', () => {
    expect(transitColor('train')).toBe('transit-train');
    expect(transitColor('cablecar')).toBe('transit-train');
    expect(transitColor('taxi')).toBe('transit-bus');
    expect(transitColor('walk')).toBe('transit-walk');
    expect(transitColor('mystery')).toBe('ink-muted');
    expect(isDashed('walk')).toBe(true);
    expect(isDashed('flight')).toBe(true);
    expect(isDashed('bus')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('builds GeoJSON lines in lng, lat order', () => {
    const features = routeFeatures(
      [{ fromKey: 'a', toKey: 'b', mode: 'walk', label: '도보', icon: '🚶', coordinates: [[34.69, 135.5], [34.7, 135.51]] }],
      { 'transit-walk': '#177a44' } as never,
    );

    expect(features.features[0]?.geometry.coordinates).toEqual([[135.5, 34.69], [135.51, 34.7]]);
    expect(features.features[0]?.properties).toEqual({ mode: 'walk', color: '#177a44', dashed: true });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 지도 스타일 구현**

`apps/web/src/lib/map/style.ts`:

```ts
import type { ColorName } from '@tripnote/design-tokens';
import type { FeatureCollection, LineString } from 'geojson';
import type { StyleSpecification } from 'maplibre-gl';
import { toLngLat } from './geo';
import type { RouteSegment } from './route';

const KOREAN_FIRST = ['coalesce', ['get', 'name:ko'], ['get', 'name']];

/** 이름을 그리는 심볼 레이어만 한국어 이름을 먼저 쓰게 바꾼다. 도로 번호와 번지는 그대로 둔다. */
export function localizeLabels(style: StyleSpecification): StyleSpecification {
  return {
    ...style,
    layers: style.layers.map((layer) => {
      if (layer.type !== 'symbol') return layer;
      const field = layer.layout?.['text-field'];
      if (field === undefined) return layer;
      const text = JSON.stringify(field);
      if (!/name/.test(text)) return layer;
      return { ...layer, layout: { ...layer.layout, 'text-field': KOREAN_FIRST as never } };
    }),
  };
}

const COLORS: Record<string, ColorName> = {
  flight: 'transit-flight',
  train: 'transit-train',
  cablecar: 'transit-train',
  bus: 'transit-bus',
  taxi: 'transit-bus',
  car: 'transit-bus',
  walk: 'transit-walk',
  boat: 'transit-boat',
};

export const transitColor = (mode: string): ColorName => COLORS[mode] ?? 'ink-muted';
export const isDashed = (mode: string) => mode === 'walk' || mode === 'flight';

export function routeFeatures(
  segments: RouteSegment[],
  palette: Record<ColorName, string>,
): FeatureCollection<LineString, { mode: string; color: string; dashed: boolean }> {
  return {
    type: 'FeatureCollection',
    features: segments.map((segment) => ({
      type: 'Feature',
      properties: { mode: segment.mode, color: palette[transitColor(segment.mode)], dashed: isDashed(segment.mode) },
      geometry: { type: 'LineString', coordinates: segment.coordinates.map(toLngLat) },
    })),
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/map/TripMap.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import 'maplibre-gl/dist/maplibre-gl.css';
import { colors } from '@tripnote/design-tokens';
import type { StyleSpecification } from 'maplibre-gl';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { Layer, Map as MapView, type MapRef, Marker, NavigationControl, Source } from 'react-map-gl/maplibre';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui';
import { boundsOf, toLngLat } from '@/lib/map/geo';
import { getPlaybackZoom, type MapPoint, pointAlongPath, type RouteSegment } from '@/lib/map/route';
import { localizeLabels, routeFeatures } from '@/lib/map/style';
import type { LatLng } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import styles from './TripMap.module.css';

const STYLE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MAP_STYLE_URL ?? 'https://tiles.openfreemap.org/styles/liberty';
const SEGMENT_MS = 2500;

function useMedia(query: string): boolean {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    (onChange) => {
      const list = window.matchMedia(query);
      list.addEventListener('change', onChange);
      return () => list.removeEventListener('change', onChange);
    },
    () => window.matchMedia(query).matches,
    () => false,
  );
}

type Props = { points: MapPoint[]; segments: RouteSegment[]; selectedKey?: string; onSelect: (key: string) => void };

export default function TripMap({ points, segments, selectedKey, onSelect }: Props) {
  const mapRef = useRef<MapRef>(null);
  const frameRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [style, setStyle] = useState<StyleSpecification | string>();
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [playhead, setPlayhead] = useState<{ at: LatLng; label: string }>();
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(true);
  const dark = useMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
  const reduceMotion = useMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
  const palette = dark ? colors.dark : colors.light;

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    fetch(STYLE_URL)
      .then((response) => response.json() as Promise<StyleSpecification>)
      .then((json) => alive && setStyle(localizeLabels(json)))
      .catch(() => alive && setStyle(STYLE_URL));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const frame = frameRef.current;
    if (!frame || typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') return;
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => setVisible(Boolean(entry?.isIntersecting)), { threshold: 0.3 });
    observer.observe(frame);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  const bounds = useMemo(() => boundsOf([...points.map((p) => p.coordinates), ...segments.flatMap((s) => s.coordinates)]), [points, segments]);
  const routes = useMemo(() => routeFeatures(segments, palette), [segments, palette]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const point = points.find((p) => p.key === selectedKey);
    if (!point) return;
    mapRef.current?.flyTo({ center: toLngLat(point.coordinates), zoom: 15, duration: reduceMotion ? 0 : 800 });
  }, [selectedKey, points, reduceMotion]);

  // 경로 재생: 구간마다 SEGMENT_MS 동안 점이 선을 따라가고 카메라가 따라간다. 보이지 않거나 움직임 줄이기면 멈춘다.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!playing || !visible || reduceMotion || !segments.length) return;
    let frame = 0;
    const started = performance.now();
    const tick = (now: number) => {
      const elapsed = (now - started) % (SEGMENT_MS * segments.length);
      const index = Math.floor(elapsed / SEGMENT_MS);
      const segment = segments[index] as RouteSegment;
      const at = pointAlongPath(segment.coordinates, (elapsed % SEGMENT_MS) / SEGMENT_MS);
      if (at) {
        const to = points.find((p) => p.key === segment.toKey);
        setPlayhead({ at, label: `${to?.title ?? ''}(으)로 이동 중 · ${segment.label}` });
        mapRef.current?.jumpTo({ center: toLngLat(at), zoom: getPlaybackZoom(segment.mode) });
      }
      frame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    frame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [playing, visible, reduceMotion, segments, points]);

  function recordCenter() {
    const center = mapRef.current?.getCenter();
    if (center && frameRef.current) frameRef.current.dataset.center = `${center.lng.toFixed(4)},${center.lat.toFixed(4)}`;
  }

  return (
    <div ref={frameRef} className={styles.frame}>
      {!style ? (
        <div className={styles.placeholder}>지도를 불러오는 중이에요.</div>
      ) : (
        <MapView
          ref={mapRef}
          mapStyle={style}
          initialViewState={bounds ? { bounds, fitBoundsOptions: { padding: 48 } } : { longitude: 127, latitude: 37.5, zoom: 4 }}
          style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
          cooperativeGestures
          dragRotate={false}
          attributionControl={{ compact: true }}
          onLoad={recordCenter}
          onMoveEnd={recordCenter}
        >
          <NavigationControl showCompass={false} position="top-right" />
          <Source id="route" type="geojson" data={routes}>
            <Layer
              id="route-solid"
              type="line"
              filter={['!=', ['get', 'dashed'], true]}
              layout={{ 'line-cap': 'round', 'line-join': 'round' }}
              paint={{ 'line-color': ['get', 'color'], 'line-width': 4 }}
            />
            <Layer
              id="route-dashed"
              type="line"
              filter={['==', ['get', 'dashed'], true]}
              layout={{ 'line-cap': 'round', 'line-join': 'round' }}
              paint={{ 'line-color': ['get', 'color'], 'line-width': 4, 'line-dasharray': [1, 2] }}
            />
          </Source>
          {points.map((point, index) => {
            const [longitude, latitude] = toLngLat(point.coordinates);
            const selected = point.key === selectedKey;
            return (
              <Marker key={point.key} longitude={longitude} latitude={latitude} anchor="center">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={[styles.pin, selected ? styles.pinSelected : '', point.approximate ? styles.approximate : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ')}
                  aria-label={`${index + 1}번째 장소 ${point.title}${point.approximate ? ' (대략 위치)' : ''}`}
                  aria-pressed={selected}
                  onClick={() => onSelect(point.key)}
                >
                  {index + 1}
                </button>
              </Marker>
            );
          })}
          {playing && playhead ? (
            <Marker longitude={toLngLat(playhead.at)[0]} latitude={toLngLat(playhead.at)[1]} anchor="center">
              <span className={styles.playhead} aria-hidden="true" />
            </Marker>
          ) : null}
        </MapView>
      )}
      {segments.length ? (
        <div className={styles.controls}>
          <span className={styles.status} role="status">
            {reduceMotion
              ? '움직임 줄이기 설정이 켜져 있어 경로 재생을 껐어요.'
              : playing && playhead
                ? playhead.label
                : `${points[0]?.title ?? ''}에서 출발`}
          </span>
          {reduceMotion ? null : (
            <Button size="sm" variant={playing ? 'secondary' : 'primary'} aria-pressed={playing} onClick={() => setPlaying((p) => !p)}>
              {playing ? '멈추기' : '경로 재생'}
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`react-map-gl`의 `Map`에 넘기는 `style`은 라이브러리 API(컨테이너 크기)라서 인라인으로 둔다. 다른 값은 모두 CSS Modules다.

`apps/web/src/components/map/DayRoute.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import type { CSSProperties } from 'react';
import { Disclosure } from '@/components/ui';
import { createGoogleDirectionsUrl, createGoogleMapsUrl, getDayMapPoints, getDayRouteSegments } from '@/lib/map/route';
import { isDashed, transitColor } from '@/lib/map/style';
import type { DayView } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import styles from './DayRoute.module.css';
import { LazyTripMap } from './LazyTripMap';

const MODE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  flight: '항공',
  train: '철도',
  cablecar: '케이블카',
  bus: '버스',
  taxi: '택시',
  car: '차량',
  walk: '도보',
  boat: '선박',
};

const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

type Props = { day: DayView; selectedKey?: string; onSelect: (key: string) => void };

export function DayRoute({ day, selectedKey, onSelect }: Props) {
  const points = getDayMapPoints(day);
  const segments = getDayRouteSegments(day);
  if (!points.length) return null;
  const byKey = new Map(points.map((p) => [p.key, p]));
  const modes = [...new Set(segments.map((s) => s.mode))];

  return (
    <section className={styles.route} aria-labelledby="day-route-title">
      <h3 id="day-route-title" className={styles.title}>
        오늘의 이동 경로
      </h3>
      <div id="day-map" role="region" aria-label={`${day.number}일차 이동 지도`}>
        <LazyTripMap points={points} segments={segments} selectedKey={selectedKey} onSelect={onSelect} />
      </div>
      <ol className={styles.order} aria-label="이날의 이동 순서">
        {points.map((point, index) => (
          <li key={point.key}>
            <button type="button" aria-pressed={point.key === selectedKey} onClick={() => onSelect(point.key)}>
              <span className={styles.number}>{pad(index + 1)}</span>
              {point.title}
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
      {modes.length ? (
        <ul className={styles.legend} aria-label="지도 선 범례">
          {modes.map((mode) => (
            <li key={mode} className={styles.chip}>
              <span
                className={[styles.line, isDashed(mode) ? styles.dashed : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ')}
                style={{ '--line-color': `var(--${transitColor(mode)})` } as CSSProperties}
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
              {MODE_LABELS[mode] ?? '이동'}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
      {segments.length ? (
        <Disclosure summary="Google 지도로 구간별 길찾기">
          <ul className={styles.directions}>
            {segments.map((segment, index) => {
              const flight = segment.mode === 'flight';
              const href = flight ? createGoogleMapsUrl(segment.coordinates.at(-1)!) : createGoogleDirectionsUrl(segment);
              return (
                <li key={`${segment.fromKey}-${segment.toKey}`}>
                  <a href={href} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                    <small>
                      {pad(index + 1)} · {segment.label}
                    </small>
                    <span>
                      {byKey.get(segment.fromKey)?.title} → {byKey.get(segment.toKey)?.title}
                    </span>
                    <span className="visually-hidden"> {flight ? '도착 공항 위치' : '길찾기'} (새 창)</span>
                  </a>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        </Disclosure>
      ) : null}
      <p className={styles.note}>지도 선은 일정의 장소와 경유지를 이은 안내 동선이에요. 실제 경로와 소요시간은 Google 길찾기에서 확인해 주세요.</p>
    </section>
  );
}
```

범례 선의 색은 토큰 이름을 CSS 변수(`--line-color`)로 넘긴다. 색 값 자체는 CSS 변수에서 온다.

### Task 10: 여행일 날씨 카드

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/weather/point.ts`, `describe.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/weather/WeatherCard.tsx`, `WeatherCard.module.css`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/trip/TripScreen.tsx` (`resources` 자리)
- Test: `apps/web/src/lib/weather/weather.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/weather/WeatherCard.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET /api/v1/weather`(Task 1), `api`(Task 3), `DayView`, `LatLng`, `WeatherResponse`, `todayIn`, `formatDayDate`(Task 5), `Icon`, `Button`(Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `weatherPointForDay(day: DayView): { label: string; coordinates: LatLng } | undefined`(`@/lib/weather/point`). 공항과 항공 스톱을 뺀 좌표의 평균, 그런 스톱이 없으면 좌표 있는 모든 스톱의 평균. 이름은 `day.city`.
  - `describeWeather(code?: number | null): { label: string; icon: IconName }`, `weatherTips(w: WeatherResponse, label: string): string[]`(`@/lib/weather/describe`).
  - `<WeatherCard day timezone />`.

카드 문구(기존 앱의 날씨 카드를 해요체로 옮김):
- 불러오는 중: "예보를 가져오는 중이에요."
- `out_of_range`, 지난 날짜: "지난 여행 날짜의 예보는 보여 드리지 않아요."
- `out_of_range`, 먼 날짜: "아직 이 날짜의 예보가 없어요. 여행 16일 전부터 볼 수 있어요."
- `unavailable` 또는 요청 실패: "날씨를 가져오지 못했어요. 잠시 후 다시 확인해 주세요." 그리고 `새로 조회` 버튼.
- `ready`: 날씨 이름, `최고 / 최저`, 최대 강수확률, 최대 풍속(`m/s`, 소수 한 자리), 팁, `Open-Meteo` 출처와 `10.16 09:00 조회`(여행 시간대 기준).
- 아래 안내: "현지 날짜 기준 예보예요. 강수확률은 하루 중 가장 높은 값이고, 먼 날짜의 예보는 달라질 수 있어요."

`apps/web/src/lib/weather/weather.test.ts`:

```ts
import { osakaTrip } from '@test/fixtures/trip';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { readTrip } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { describeWeather, weatherTips } from './describe';
import { weatherPointForDay } from './point';

describe('weatherPointForDay', () => {
  const days = readTrip(osakaTrip()).days;

  it('averages the day stops on the ground and names the city', () => {
    const point = weatherPointForDay(days[1]!);
    expect(point?.label).toBe(days[1]!.city);
    const [lat, lng] = point!.coordinates;
    expect(lat).toBeGreaterThan(34.5);
    expect(lat).toBeLessThan(35.2);
    expect(lng).toBeGreaterThan(135.4);
    expect(lng).toBeLessThan(135.9);
  });

  it('leaves out airports and flights so day 1 is not pulled toward the sea', () => {
    const ground = days[0]!.stops.filter((s) => s.coordinates && !['airport', 'flight'].includes(s.type));
    const lat = ground.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.coordinates![0], 0) / ground.length;
    expect(weatherPointForDay(days[0]!)?.coordinates[0]).toBeCloseTo(lat, 6);
  });

  it('has no point for a day without coordinates', () => {
    expect(weatherPointForDay({ ...days[0]!, stops: days[0]!.stops.map((s) => ({ ...s, coordinates: undefined })) })).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('describeWeather and weatherTips', () => {
  it('names Open-Meteo weather codes', () => {
    expect(describeWeather(0)).toEqual({ label: '맑음', icon: 'weather-sun' });
    expect(describeWeather(2).label).toBe('구름 조금');
    expect(describeWeather(63)).toEqual({ label: '비', icon: 'weather-rain' });
    expect(describeWeather(75).label).toBe('눈');
    expect(describeWeather(95).label).toBe('뇌우');
    expect(describeWeather(undefined).label).toBe('날씨 정보 없음');
  });

  it('suggests rain gear and a jacket when needed', () => {
    const base = { date: '2026-10-21', status: 'ready', condition: 63, high: 22, low: 15, rainChance: 70, windSpeed: 4.3, checkedAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z' };
    expect(weatherTips(base, '비')).toEqual(['우산이나 우비를 챙겨요. 실내에서 쉴 곳도 생각해 둬요.', '아침저녁용 겉옷을 챙겨요.']);
    expect(weatherTips({ ...base, rainChance: 10, low: 20 }, '맑음')).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/components/weather/WeatherCard.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { osakaTrip } from '@test/fixtures/trip';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { readTrip } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { WeatherCard } from './WeatherCard';

const GET = vi.fn();
vi.mock('@/lib/api/client', () => ({ api: { GET: (...args: unknown[]) => GET(...args) } }));

const day = readTrip(osakaTrip()).days[0]!;

function renderCard() {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={client}>
      <WeatherCard day={day} timezone="Asia/Tokyo" />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

const ok = (data: object) => ({ data, error: undefined, response: new Response(null, { status: 200 }) });

describe('WeatherCard', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    GET.mockReset();
    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'], now: new Date('2026-10-16T03:00:00Z') });
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('asks the api for the day point in the trip time zone', async () => {
    GET.mockResolvedValue(ok({ date: day.date, status: 'out_of_range' }));
    renderCard();
    await screen.findByText(/아직 이 날짜의 예보가 없어요/);

    const [path, options] = GET.mock.calls[0]!;
    expect(path).toBe('/api/v1/weather');
    expect(options.params.query).toMatchObject({ date: day.date, timezone: 'Asia/Tokyo' });
  });

  it('shows the forecast with tips and the source', async () => {
    GET.mockResolvedValue(
      ok({ date: day.date, status: 'ready', condition: 63, high: 22.4, low: 15.1, rainChance: 70, windSpeed: 4.25, checkedAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z' }),
    );
    renderCard();

    expect(await screen.findByText('비')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('22° / 15°')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('70%')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('4.3 m/s')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/우산이나 우비를 챙겨요/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: /Open-Meteo/ })).toHaveAttribute('href', 'https://open-meteo.com/');
    expect(screen.getByText(/10\.16 09:00 조회/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('explains past dates', async () => {
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date('2026-12-01T00:00:00Z'));
    GET.mockResolvedValue(ok({ date: day.date, status: 'out_of_range' }));
    renderCard();
    expect(await screen.findByText('지난 여행 날짜의 예보는 보여 드리지 않아요.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('offers a retry when the forecast is unavailable', async () => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
    GET.mockResolvedValueOnce(ok({ date: day.date, status: 'unavailable' })).mockResolvedValueOnce(
      ok({ date: day.date, status: 'ready', condition: 0, high: 20, low: 18, rainChance: 0, windSpeed: 1, checkedAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z' }),
    );
    renderCard();

    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: '새로 조회' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('맑음')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(GET).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it('treats a failed request like an unavailable forecast', async () => {
    GET.mockRejectedValue(new TypeError('fetch failed'));
    renderCard();
    expect(await screen.findByText('날씨를 가져오지 못했어요. 잠시 후 다시 확인해 주세요.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`apps/web/src/lib/weather/point.ts`:

```ts
import type { DayView, LatLng } from '@/lib/trip/content';

const average = (points: LatLng[]): LatLng => [
  points.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p[0], 0) / points.length,
  points.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p[1], 0) / points.length,
];

export function weatherPointForDay(day: DayView): { label: string; coordinates: LatLng } | undefined {
  const located = day.stops.filter((s) => s.coordinates);
  const ground = located.filter((s) => s.type !== 'airport' && s.type !== 'flight');
  const pick = (ground.length ? ground : located).map((s) => s.coordinates as LatLng);
  return pick.length ? { label: day.city, coordinates: average(pick) } : undefined;
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/weather/describe.ts`:

```ts
import type { IconName } from '@/components/ui/icons.generated';
import type { WeatherResponse } from '@/lib/trip/content';

const RAINY = new Set(['이슬비', '비', '소나기', '뇌우']);

export function describeWeather(code?: number | null): { label: string; icon: IconName } {
  if (code === 0 || code === 1) return { label: '맑음', icon: 'weather-sun' };
  if (code === 2) return { label: '구름 조금', icon: 'weather-partly' };
  if (code === 3) return { label: '흐림', icon: 'weather-cloud' };
  if (code === 45 || code === 48) return { label: '안개', icon: 'weather-cloud' };
  if (code !== undefined && code !== null && [51, 53, 55, 56, 57].includes(code)) return { label: '이슬비', icon: 'weather-rain' };
  if (code !== undefined && code !== null && [61, 63, 65, 66, 67].includes(code)) return { label: '비', icon: 'weather-rain' };
  if (code !== undefined && code !== null && [80, 81, 82].includes(code)) return { label: '소나기', icon: 'weather-rain' };
  if (code !== undefined && code !== null && [71, 73, 75, 77, 85, 86].includes(code)) return { label: '눈', icon: 'weather-snow' };
  if (code !== undefined && code !== null && [95, 96, 97, 99].includes(code)) return { label: '뇌우', icon: 'weather-rain' };
  return { label: '날씨 정보 없음', icon: 'weather-cloud' };
}

export function weatherTips(w: WeatherResponse, label: string): string[] {
  const tips: string[] = [];
  if ((typeof w.rainChance === 'number' && w.rainChance >= 50) || RAINY.has(label)) {
    tips.push('우산이나 우비를 챙겨요. 실내에서 쉴 곳도 생각해 둬요.');
  }
  if (typeof w.low === 'number' && w.low < 18) tips.push('아침저녁용 겉옷을 챙겨요.');
  return tips;
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/weather/WeatherCard.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Button, Icon } from '@/components/ui';
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import type { DayView, WeatherResponse } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { todayIn } from '@/lib/trip/day';
import { formatDayDate } from '@/lib/trip/format';
import { describeWeather, weatherTips } from '@/lib/weather/describe';
import { weatherPointForDay } from '@/lib/weather/point';
import styles from './WeatherCard.module.css';

const FAILED = '날씨를 가져오지 못했어요. 잠시 후 다시 확인해 주세요.';
const round = (v?: number | null) => (typeof v === 'number' ? `${Math.round(v)}°` : '–');

function checkedLabel(iso: string, timezone: string) {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { timeZone: timezone, month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false })
    .formatToParts(new Date(iso))
    .reduce<Record<string, string>>((acc, p) => ({ ...acc, [p.type]: p.value }), {});
  return `${parts.month}.${parts.day} ${parts.hour}:${parts.minute} 조회`;
}

export function WeatherCard({ day, timezone }: { day: DayView; timezone: string }) {
  const point = weatherPointForDay(day);
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['weather', point?.coordinates[0], point?.coordinates[1], day.date, timezone],
    enabled: Boolean(point),
    staleTime: 30 * 60 * 1000,
    queryFn: async (): Promise<WeatherResponse> => {
      const [lat, lng] = point!.coordinates;
      const { data, error } = await api.GET('/api/v1/weather', { params: { query: { lat, lng, date: day.date, timezone } } });
      if (!data) throw error ?? new Error('weather failed');
      return data;
    },
  });
  if (!point) return null;

  const weather = query.data;
  const description = describeWeather(weather?.condition);
  const failed = query.isError || weather?.status === 'unavailable';

  return (
    <section className={styles.card} aria-labelledby={`weather-${day.number}`}>
      <div className={styles.head}>
        <h3 id={`weather-${day.number}`} className={styles.title}>
          <Icon name={weather?.status === 'ready' ? description.icon : 'weather-partly'} />
          {point.label} 여행일 날씨
        </h3>
        <span className={styles.date}>{formatDayDate(day.date)} 예상</span>
      </div>

      {query.isPending ? <p className={styles.empty}>예보를 가져오는 중이에요.</p> : null}

      {failed ? (
        <>
          <p className={styles.empty}>{FAILED}</p>
          <Button size="sm" onClick={() => query.refetch()} disabled={query.isFetching}>
            새로 조회
          </Button>
        </>
      ) : null}

      {weather?.status === 'out_of_range' ? (
        <p className={styles.empty}>
          {day.date < todayIn(timezone) ? '지난 여행 날짜의 예보는 보여 드리지 않아요.' : '아직 이 날짜의 예보가 없어요. 여행 16일 전부터 볼 수 있어요.'}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {weather?.status === 'ready' ? (
        <>
          <div className={styles.summary}>
            <strong className={styles.condition}>{description.label}</strong>
            <span>
              <span className={styles.temps}>
                {round(weather.high)} / {round(weather.low)}
              </span>{' '}
              <span className={styles.date}>최고 / 최저</span>
            </span>
          </div>
          <dl className={styles.metrics}>
            <div>
              <dt>최대 강수확률</dt>
              <dd>{typeof weather.rainChance === 'number' ? `${weather.rainChance}%` : '제공 없음'}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>최대 풍속</dt>
              <dd>{typeof weather.windSpeed === 'number' ? `${weather.windSpeed.toFixed(1)} m/s` : '제공 없음'}</dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
          {weatherTips(weather, description.label).map((tip) => (
            <p key={tip} className={styles.tip}>
              {tip}
            </p>
          ))}
          <div className={styles.source}>
            <a href="https://open-meteo.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
              Open-Meteo<span className="visually-hidden"> (새 창)</span>
            </a>
            {weather.checkedAt ? <span>{checkedLabel(weather.checkedAt, timezone)}</span> : null}
          </div>
        </>
      ) : null}

      <p className={styles.note}>현지 날짜 기준 예보예요. 강수확률은 하루 중 가장 높은 값이고, 먼 날짜의 예보는 달라질 수 있어요.</p>
    </section>
  );
}
```

테스트의 `22° / 15°`는 `.temps` 요소 하나의 글자다(`{round(high)} / {round(low)}`가 한 요소 안에 있다).

- [ ] **Step 4: TripScreen에 연결**

`apps/web/src/components/trip/TripScreen.tsx`에 `import { WeatherCard } from '@/components/weather/WeatherCard';`를 더하고, `resources`의 `{/* Task 10: 날씨 카드 */}`를 `<WeatherCard day={day} timezone={trip.timezone} />`로 바꾼다.

`TripScreen`은 이제 `useQuery`를 쓰므로, `TripScreen.test.tsx`와 `TripSections.test.tsx`의 `render(...)`를 `QueryClientProvider`로 감싼다. 두 파일 맨 위에 도우미를 두고 `render(` 호출을 `renderScreen(`으로 바꾼다:

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';

vi.mock('@/lib/api/client', () => ({ api: { GET: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: { date: '2026-10-21', status: 'out_of_range' } })) } }));

function renderScreen(ui: ReactElement) {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(<QueryClientProvider client={client}>{ui}</QueryClientProvider>);
}
```

### Task 11: 홈 화면

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/trip/home.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/home/TripCard.tsx`, `TripCard.module.css`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/home/HomeSections.tsx`, `Home.module.css`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `SiteHeader.module.css`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/app/page.tsx`
- Test: `apps/web/src/lib/trip/home.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/home/Home.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createServerApi`, `serverApi`(Task 3), `TripSummary`, `MeResponse`, `readSummaryCover`, `formatDateRange`, `tripLength`(Task 5), `Badge`, `Button`, `EmptyState`(Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `fetchSamples(api): Promise<TripSummary[] | null>`(실패면 `null`), `fetchAccount(api): Promise<{ me: MeResponse; trips: TripSummary[] } | null>`(로그인하지 않았으면 `null`)(`@/lib/trip/home`).
  - `<TripCard trip: TripSummary />`: 홈과 이후 내 여행 목록이 같이 쓴다. 표지 사진 16:9, 제목(`display`), 기간, 부제 또는 도시, 스톱 수, 샘플 배지. 표지가 없으면 `hero-route` 일러스트.
  - `<SampleList samples: TripSummary[] | null />`, `<MyTrips account: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof fetchAccount>> />`.
  - `<SiteHeader />`(서버): 로고, 로그인 상태에 따라 `로그인` 또는 `내 정보` 링크. Task 12의 인증 화면도 쓴다.

캐시 규칙(ADR 0006, `cacheComponents`): 공개 샘플 목록은 쿠키 없이 부르는 `'use cache'` 함수로 몇 분 동안 캐시한다. 실패는 캐시하지 않는다(함수 안에서 던지고 밖에서 받는다). 로그인 상태가 필요한 부분은 `<Suspense>` 안에서 요청마다 그린다. 정적 껍데기(소개 문구)는 빌드할 때 그린다. 빌드 중에는 API가 없으므로 샘플 목록은 `await connection()` 뒤에서 부른다.

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/home.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createServerApi } from '@/lib/api/server-client';
import { fetchAccount, fetchSamples } from './home';

const summary = { slug: 'danang-hoian-family-2027', title: '다낭·호이안 가족여행', startDate: '2027-03-03', endDate: '2027-03-07', isSample: true, visibility: 'public', cities: ['DA NANG'], stopCount: 57 };

function apiWith(routes: Record<string, () => Response | Promise<Response>>) {
  const fetch = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL) => {
    const path = new URL((input as Request).url).pathname;
    const route = routes[path];
    return route ? route() : new Response(null, { status: 404 });
  });
  return createServerApi('__Host-SESSION=abc', { baseUrl: 'http://api.test', fetch });
}

describe('fetchSamples', () => {
  it('returns the public samples', async () => {
    expect(await fetchSamples(apiWith({ '/api/v1/samples': () => Response.json([summary]) }))).toEqual([summary]);
  });

  it('returns null when the api fails', async () => {
    expect(await fetchSamples(apiWith({ '/api/v1/samples': () => new Response(null, { status: 503 }) }))).toBeNull();
    expect(await fetchSamples(apiWith({ '/api/v1/samples': () => Promise.reject(new TypeError('down')) }))).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('fetchAccount', () => {
  it('returns null for a visitor who is not signed in', async () => {
    expect(await fetchAccount(apiWith({ '/api/v1/me': () => Response.json({ detail: '로그인이 필요해요.' }, { status: 401 }) }))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('returns the profile and their trips', async () => {
    const me = { id: 'u1', email: 'mina@example.com', nickname: '미나' };
    const account = await fetchAccount(
      apiWith({ '/api/v1/me': () => Response.json(me), '/api/v1/trips': () => Response.json([{ ...summary, isSample: false, visibility: 'private' }]) }),
    );
    expect(account?.me).toEqual(me);
    expect(account?.trips).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { TripSummary } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { MyTrips, SampleList } from './HomeSections';
import { TripCard } from './TripCard';

const danang: TripSummary = {
  slug: 'danang-hoian-family-2027',
  title: '다낭·호이안 가족여행',
  subtitle: '엄마·아빠·초등학생 아이가 함께 떠나는 3박 5일 자유여행',
  startDate: '2027-03-03',
  endDate: '2027-03-07',
  isSample: true,
  visibility: 'public',
  cities: ['DA NANG', 'HOI AN → DA NANG'],
  stopCount: 57,
  coverPhoto: { src: 'https://upload.wikimedia.org/a.jpg', alt: '호이안 올드타운 등불', credit: 'Mig Gilbert' },
};

describe('TripCard', () => {
  it('links to the itinerary with its cover, dates and size', () => {
    render(<TripCard trip={danang} />);
    const link = screen.getByRole('link', { name: /다낭·호이안 가족여행/ });

    expect(link).toHaveAttribute('href', '/t/danang-hoian-family-2027');
    expect(within(link).getByRole('img', { name: '호이안 올드타운 등불' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(link).getByText('2027.03.03 – 03.07 · 5일')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(link).getByText(danang.subtitle!)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(link).getByText('스톱 57개')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(link).getByText('샘플')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('falls back to the route illustration without a cover', () => {
    const { container } = render(<TripCard trip={{ ...danang, coverPhoto: undefined, subtitle: undefined }} />);
    expect(container.querySelector('img')).toHaveAttribute('src', '/ds/illustrations/hero-route.svg');
    expect(container.querySelector('img')).toHaveAttribute('alt', '');
    expect(screen.getByText('DA NANG · HOI AN → DA NANG')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('SampleList', () => {
  it('shows cards, an empty message or a failure message', () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<SampleList samples={[danang]} />);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('link', { name: /다낭/ })).toHaveLength(1);

    rerender(<SampleList samples={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('지금은 공개된 샘플 일정이 없어요.')).toBeInTheDocument();

    rerender(<SampleList samples={null} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('샘플 일정을 불러오지 못했어요. 잠시 후 새로고침해 주세요.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('MyTrips', () => {
  it('invites a visitor to sign in', () => {
    render(<MyTrips account={null} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: '로그인하기' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '/login');
  });

  it('greets a member and lists their trips or an empty state', () => {
    const me = { id: 'u1', email: 'mina@example.com', nickname: '미나' };
    const { rerender } = render(<MyTrips account={{ me, trips: [{ ...danang, isSample: false }] }} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: '미나님의 여행' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('link', { name: /다낭/ })).toHaveLength(1);

    rerender(<MyTrips account={{ me, trips: [] }} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('아직 만든 여행이 없어요')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/trip/home.ts`:

```ts
import type { Client } from 'openapi-fetch';
import type { paths } from '@/lib/api/schema';
import type { MeResponse, TripSummary } from './content';

export async function fetchSamples(api: Client<paths>): Promise<TripSummary[] | null> {
  try {
    const { data } = await api.GET('/api/v1/samples');
    return data ?? null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function fetchAccount(api: Client<paths>): Promise<{ me: MeResponse; trips: TripSummary[] } | null> {
  try {
    const { data: me } = await api.GET('/api/v1/me');
    if (!me) return null;
    const { data: trips } = await api.GET('/api/v1/trips');
    return { me, trips: trips ?? [] };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/home/TripCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui';
import { readSummaryCover, type TripSummary } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import { formatDateRange, tripLength } from '@/lib/trip/format';
import styles from './TripCard.module.css';

export function TripCard({ trip }: { trip: TripSummary }) {
  const cover = readSummaryCover(trip);
  return (
    <Link href={`/t/${trip.slug}`} className={styles.card}>
      <div className={styles.cover}>
        {/* biome-ignore lint/performance/noImgElement: 표지는 외부 출처 사진이라 next/image 원격 설정 전까지 img를 쓴다 */}
        <img src={cover?.src ?? '/ds/illustrations/hero-route.svg'} alt={cover?.alt ?? ''} width={640} height={360} loading="lazy" />
      </div>
      <div className={styles.body}>
        <h3 className={styles.title}>{trip.title}</h3>
        <span className={styles.dates}>
          {formatDateRange(trip.startDate, trip.endDate)} · {tripLength(trip.startDate, trip.endDate)}
        </span>
        <p className={styles.subtitle}>{trip.subtitle ?? trip.cities.slice(0, 3).join(' · ')}</p>
        <div className={styles.foot}>
          <span>스톱 {trip.stopCount}개</span>
          {trip.isSample ? <Badge tone="brand">샘플</Badge> : null}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Button, EmptyState } from '@/components/ui';
import type { MeResponse, TripSummary } from '@/lib/trip/content';
import styles from './Home.module.css';
import { TripCard } from './TripCard';

export function SampleList({ samples }: { samples: TripSummary[] | null }) {
  return (
    <section id="samples" className={styles.section} aria-labelledby="samples-title">
      <h2 id="samples-title" className={styles.sectionTitle}>
        샘플 일정
      </h2>
      {samples === null ? <p className={styles.message}>샘플 일정을 불러오지 못했어요. 잠시 후 새로고침해 주세요.</p> : null}
      {samples?.length === 0 ? <p className={styles.message}>지금은 공개된 샘플 일정이 없어요.</p> : null}
      {samples?.length ? (
        <div className={styles.cards}>
          {samples.map((trip) => (
            <TripCard key={trip.slug} trip={trip} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

type Account = { me: MeResponse; trips: TripSummary[] } | null;

export function MyTrips({ account }: { account: Account }) {
  if (!account) {
    return (
      <section className={styles.section} aria-labelledby="my-trips-title">
        <h2 id="my-trips-title" className={styles.sectionTitle}>
          내 여행
        </h2>
        <p className={styles.message}>로그인하면 내가 만든 여행과 복제한 여행을 여기서 볼 수 있어요.</p>
        <div className={styles.actions}>
          <Button href="/login" variant="primary">
            로그인하기
          </Button>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }
  return (
    <section className={styles.section} aria-labelledby="my-trips-title">
      <h2 id="my-trips-title" className={styles.sectionTitle}>
        {account.me.nickname}님의 여행
      </h2>
      {account.trips.length ? (
        <div className={styles.cards}>
          {account.trips.map((trip) => (
            <TripCard key={trip.slug} trip={trip} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <EmptyState
          illustration="empty-itinerary"
          title="아직 만든 여행이 없어요"
          text="여행 만들기는 곧 열려요. 먼저 샘플 일정을 둘러보세요."
          action={<Button href="#samples">샘플 일정 보기</Button>}
          headingLevel={3}
        />
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui';
import { serverApi } from '@/lib/api/server';
import styles from './SiteHeader.module.css';

async function AccountLink() {
  const api = await serverApi();
  const { data: me } = await api.GET('/api/v1/me').catch(() => ({ data: undefined }));
  return me ? (
    <Button href="/me" size="sm" variant="ghost">
      내 정보
    </Button>
  ) : (
    <Button href="/login" size="sm" variant="secondary">
      로그인
    </Button>
  );
}

export function SiteHeader() {
  return (
    <header className={styles.header}>
      <div className={styles.inner}>
        <Link href="/" className={styles.brand} aria-label="여행노트 홈">
          {/* biome-ignore lint/performance/noImgElement: 로고 SVG */}
          <img src="/brand/lockup-horizontal.svg" alt="" width={120} height={28} />
        </Link>
        <Suspense fallback={null}>
          <AccountLink />
        </Suspense>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import { cacheLife, cacheTag } from 'next/cache';
import { connection } from 'next/server';
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { MyTrips, SampleList } from '@/components/home/HomeSections';
import styles from '@/components/home/Home.module.css';
import { SiteHeader } from '@/components/layout/SiteHeader';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui';
import { serverApi } from '@/lib/api/server';
import { createServerApi } from '@/lib/api/server-client';
import { fetchAccount, fetchSamples } from '@/lib/trip/home';

async function cachedSamples() {
  'use cache';
  cacheLife('minutes');
  cacheTag('samples');
  const samples = await fetchSamples(createServerApi(''));
  // 실패를 캐시하지 않도록 던진다. 밖에서 받아 안내 문구로 바꾼다.
  if (!samples) throw new Error('samples unavailable');
  return samples;
}

async function Samples() {
  await connection();
  const samples = await cachedSamples().catch(() => null);
  return <SampleList samples={samples} />;
}

async function Account() {
  return <MyTrips account={await fetchAccount(await serverApi())} />;
}

export default function Home() {
  return (
    <>
      <SiteHeader />
      <main id="main" className="page">
        <section className={styles.hero} aria-labelledby="home-title">
          <h1 id="home-title" className={styles.headline}>
            예약은 어디서든, 일정은 여기서.
          </h1>
          <p className={styles.lead}>여행 당일 손에 들고 보는 일정표예요. 시간과 동선, 메모와 준비물을 한 화면에서 확인해요.</p>
          <div className={styles.actions}>
            <Button href="#samples" variant="primary">
              샘플 일정 보기
            </Button>
          </div>
        </section>
        <Suspense fallback={<p className={styles.message}>내 여행을 불러오는 중이에요.</p>}>
          <Account />
        </Suspense>
        <Suspense fallback={<p className={styles.message}>샘플 일정을 불러오는 중이에요.</p>}>
          <Samples />
        </Suspense>
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```

### Task 12: 로그인, 가입, 이메일 인증, 비밀번호 재설정, 내 정보

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/auth/schemas.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/auth/LoginForm.tsx`, `SignupForm.tsx`, `VerifyEmailForm.tsx`, `ResetForms.tsx`, `AccountActions.tsx`, `Auth.module.css`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/layout.tsx`, `(auth)/login/page.tsx`, `(auth)/signup/page.tsx`, `(auth)/verify-email/page.tsx`, `(auth)/reset-password/page.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/me/page.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/app/globals.css` (`.page-narrow`)
- Test: `apps/web/src/lib/auth/schemas.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/auth/auth.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `api`, `problemMessage`(Task 3), `serverApi`(Task 3), `TextField`, `Button`(Task 4), `SiteHeader`(Task 11). API: `POST /api/v1/auth/login|signup|verify-email|verify-email/resend|password-reset|password-reset/confirm|logout`, `GET|DELETE /api/v1/me`, `GET /oauth2/authorization/google`. 구글 실패는 `/login?error=google`로 돌아온다.
- Produces: 화면 `/login`, `/signup`, `/verify-email?token=`, `/reset-password`, `/reset-password?token=`, `/me`. 스키마 `loginSchema`, `signupSchema`, `verifySchema`, `emailSchema`, `resetSchema`(`@/lib/auth/schemas`).

규칙(설계 3.7, ADR 0009, 2번 계획의 판단):
- 클라이언트 검증은 API와 같은 한도를 쓴다. 이메일 254자, 닉네임 1~30자, 비밀번호 10~128자. 유출 비밀번호 검사는 서버만 한다.
- 가입과 재설정 요청 응답은 계정이 있는지 드러내지 않는다. 서버의 `message`를 그대로 보여 준다.
- 이메일 인증 화면은 가입할 때 정한 비밀번호를 다시 받는다(2번 계획에서 가입 탈취를 막으려고 정한 규칙).
- 구글 버튼은 `<a href="/oauth2/authorization/google">`이다. 전체 페이지 이동이어야 OAuth 리다이렉트가 동작한다.
- 탈퇴는 브라우저 확인 창을 쓰지 않는다. 확인 칸에 `탈퇴`를 입력해야 버튼이 켜진다.
- 비밀번호 칸은 `autoComplete`(`current-password`, `new-password`)를 단다.

`apps/web/src/lib/auth/schemas.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { loginSchema, resetSchema, signupSchema } from './schemas';

const issues = (result: { success: boolean; error?: { issues: { path: PropertyKey[]; message: string }[] } }) =>
  Object.fromEntries((result.error?.issues ?? []).map((i) => [String(i.path[0]), i.message]));

describe('auth schemas', () => {
  it('explains a wrong email and an empty password', () => {
    expect(issues(loginSchema.safeParse({ email: 'mina', password: '' }))).toEqual({
      email: '이메일 주소를 확인해 주세요.',
      password: '비밀번호를 입력해 주세요.',
    });
  });

  it('uses the api limits for signup', () => {
    expect(issues(signupSchema.safeParse({ nickname: ' ', email: 'mina@example.com', password: 'short' }))).toEqual({
      nickname: '닉네임을 입력해 주세요.',
      password: '비밀번호는 10자 이상이어야 해요.',
    });
    expect(issues(signupSchema.safeParse({ nickname: '가'.repeat(31), email: 'mina@example.com', password: 'x'.repeat(129) }))).toEqual({
      nickname: '닉네임은 30자까지 쓸 수 있어요.',
      password: '비밀번호는 128자 이하여야 해요.',
    });
    expect(signupSchema.safeParse({ nickname: '미나', email: 'mina@example.com', password: 'trip-note-2027' }).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('needs the new password twice', () => {
    expect(issues(resetSchema.safeParse({ password: 'trip-note-2027', confirm: 'trip-note-2028' }))).toEqual({
      confirm: '두 비밀번호가 같지 않아요.',
    });
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/components/auth/auth.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { DeleteAccount, LogoutButton } from './AccountActions';
import { LoginForm } from './LoginForm';
import { ResetConfirmForm, ResetRequestForm } from './ResetForms';
import { SignupForm } from './SignupForm';
import { VerifyEmailForm } from './VerifyEmailForm';

const router = { replace: vi.fn(), refresh: vi.fn(), push: vi.fn() };
vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({ useRouter: () => router }));

const POST = vi.fn();
const DELETE = vi.fn();
vi.mock('@/lib/api/client', () => ({
  api: { POST: (...args: unknown[]) => POST(...args), DELETE: (...args: unknown[]) => DELETE(...args) },
}));

const reply = (status: number, body?: object) => ({
  data: status < 300 ? body : undefined,
  error: status >= 300 ? body : undefined,
  response: new Response(null, { status: status === 204 ? 204 : status }),
});

beforeEach(() => {
  POST.mockReset();
  DELETE.mockReset();
  for (const fn of Object.values(router)) fn.mockReset();
});

describe('LoginForm', () => {
  it('validates before calling the api', async () => {
    render(<LoginForm googleError={false} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '로그인하기' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('이메일 주소를 확인해 주세요.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(POST).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('signs in and goes home', async () => {
    POST.mockResolvedValue(reply(200, { id: 'u1', email: 'mina@example.com', nickname: '미나' }));
    render(<LoginForm googleError={false} />);

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('이메일'), 'mina@example.com');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('비밀번호'), 'trip-note-2027');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '로그인하기' }));

    expect(POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/v1/auth/login', { body: { email: 'mina@example.com', password: 'trip-note-2027' } });
    expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/');
    expect(router.refresh).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('shows the server reason when sign-in fails', async () => {
    POST.mockResolvedValue(reply(401, { detail: '이메일 또는 비밀번호가 맞지 않아요.' }));
    render(<LoginForm googleError={false} />);

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('이메일'), 'mina@example.com');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('비밀번호'), 'wrong-password');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '로그인하기' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('이메일 또는 비밀번호가 맞지 않아요.');
    expect(router.replace).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('offers google as a full page link and explains a failed google sign-in', () => {
    render(<LoginForm googleError />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: '구글로 계속하기' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '/oauth2/authorization/google');
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('구글 로그인을 마치지 못했어요. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
  });
});

describe('SignupForm', () => {
  it('shows the neutral server message after sending', async () => {
    POST.mockResolvedValue(reply(202, { message: '가입된 이메일이면 확인 메일을 보냈어요.' }));
    render(<SignupForm />);

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(/닉네임/), '미나');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(/이메일/), 'mina@example.com');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(/비밀번호/), 'trip-note-2027');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '가입하기' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('가입된 이메일이면 확인 메일을 보냈어요.');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: '가입하기' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('VerifyEmailForm', () => {
  it('sends the token with the password chosen at signup', async () => {
    POST.mockResolvedValue(reply(200, { message: '가입이 끝났어요.' }));
    render(<VerifyEmailForm token="tok-123" />);

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('가입할 때 정한 비밀번호'), 'trip-note-2027');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '가입 마치기' }));

    expect(POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/v1/auth/verify-email', { body: { token: 'tok-123', password: 'trip-note-2027' } });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: '로그인하기' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '/login');
  });

  it('shows why a link no longer works', async () => {
    POST.mockResolvedValue(reply(400, { detail: '인증 링크가 만료됐어요. 메일을 다시 받아 주세요.' }));
    render(<VerifyEmailForm token="old" />);

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('가입할 때 정한 비밀번호'), 'trip-note-2027');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '가입 마치기' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('인증 링크가 만료됐어요.');
  });
});

describe('ResetForms', () => {
  it('requests a reset link', async () => {
    POST.mockResolvedValue(reply(202, { message: '가입된 이메일이면 재설정 메일을 보냈어요.' }));
    render(<ResetRequestForm />);

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('이메일'), 'mina@example.com');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '재설정 메일 받기' }));

    expect(POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/v1/auth/password-reset', { body: { email: 'mina@example.com' } });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('재설정 메일을 보냈어요');
  });

  it('checks the two new passwords match before sending', async () => {
    render(<ResetConfirmForm token="tok" />);

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('새 비밀번호'), 'trip-note-2027');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('새 비밀번호 확인'), 'trip-note-2028');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '비밀번호 바꾸기' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('두 비밀번호가 같지 않아요.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(POST).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('AccountActions', () => {
  it('logs out and refreshes', async () => {
    POST.mockResolvedValue(reply(204));
    render(<LogoutButton />);

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '로그아웃' }));

    expect(POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/v1/auth/logout');
    expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/');
    expect(router.refresh).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('deletes the account only after typing the confirmation word', async () => {
    DELETE.mockResolvedValue(reply(204));
    render(<DeleteAccount />);

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '탈퇴하기' }));
    const confirm = screen.getByRole('button', { name: '계정과 여행 모두 지우기' });
    expect(confirm).toBeDisabled();

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('확인을 위해 탈퇴를 입력해 주세요'), '탈퇴');
    await userEvent.click(confirm);

    expect(DELETE).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/v1/me');
    expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/');
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/auth/schemas.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';

const EMAIL = '이메일 주소를 확인해 주세요.';

const email = z.email(EMAIL).max(254, EMAIL);
const newPassword = z.string().min(10, '비밀번호는 10자 이상이어야 해요.').max(128, '비밀번호는 128자 이하여야 해요.');

export const loginSchema = z.object({ email, password: z.string().min(1, '비밀번호를 입력해 주세요.') });
export const signupSchema = z.object({
  nickname: z.string().trim().min(1, '닉네임을 입력해 주세요.').max(30, '닉네임은 30자까지 쓸 수 있어요.'),
  email,
  password: newPassword,
});
export const verifySchema = z.object({ password: z.string().min(1, '가입할 때 정한 비밀번호를 입력해 주세요.') });
export const emailSchema = z.object({ email });
export const resetSchema = z
  .object({ password: newPassword, confirm: z.string() })
  .refine((v) => v.password === v.confirm, { path: ['confirm'], message: '두 비밀번호가 같지 않아요.' });

export type LoginValues = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;
export type SignupValues = z.infer<typeof signupSchema>;
export type VerifyValues = z.infer<typeof verifySchema>;
export type EmailValues = z.infer<typeof emailSchema>;
export type ResetValues = z.infer<typeof resetSchema>;
```

```css
  text-align: center;
  font: var(--text-caption);
  color: var(--ink-muted);
}

.error {
  margin: 0;
  padding: var(--space-3);
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--danger-soft);
  color: var(--danger);
}

.done {
  margin: 0;
  padding: var(--space-3);
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--success-soft);
  color: var(--success);
}

.links {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: var(--space-3);
  margin: 0;
  font: var(--text-body-sm);
}

.links a {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  min-height: var(--control-md);
}

.danger {
  display: grid;
  gap: var(--space-3);
  padding: var(--space-4);
  border: 1px solid var(--danger);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/auth/LoginForm.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Button, TextField } from '@/components/ui';
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { problemMessage } from '@/lib/api/errors';
import { type LoginValues, loginSchema } from '@/lib/auth/schemas';
import styles from './Auth.module.css';

export function LoginForm({ googleError }: { googleError: boolean }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string>();
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState } = useForm<LoginValues>({ resolver: zodResolver(loginSchema) });

  async function onSubmit(values: LoginValues) {
    setServerError(undefined);
    try {
      const { response, error } = await api.POST('/api/v1/auth/login', { body: values });
      if (response.ok) {
        router.replace('/');
        router.refresh();
        return;
      }
      setServerError(problemMessage(error, response.status));
    } catch (e) {
      setServerError(problemMessage(e));
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate>
      <h1 className={styles.title}>로그인</h1>
      {googleError ? (
        <p role="alert" className={styles.error}>
          구글 로그인을 마치지 못했어요. 다시 시도해 주세요.
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <a className={styles.google} href="/oauth2/authorization/google">
        구글로 계속하기
      </a>
      <p className={styles.divider}>또는 이메일로</p>
      <TextField label="이메일" type="email" autoComplete="email" error={formState.errors.email?.message} {...register('email')} />
      <TextField
        label="비밀번호"
        type="password"
        autoComplete="current-password"
        error={formState.errors.password?.message}
        {...register('password')}
      />
      {serverError ? (
        <p role="alert" className={styles.error}>
          {serverError}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="lg" disabled={formState.isSubmitting}>
        로그인하기
      </Button>
      <p className={styles.links}>
        <Link href="/signup">이메일로 가입하기</Link>
        <Link href="/reset-password">비밀번호 찾기</Link>
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/auth/SignupForm.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Button, TextField } from '@/components/ui';
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { problemMessage } from '@/lib/api/errors';
import { type SignupValues, signupSchema } from '@/lib/auth/schemas';
import styles from './Auth.module.css';

export function SignupForm() {
  const [sent, setSent] = useState<string>();
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string>();
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState } = useForm<SignupValues>({ resolver: zodResolver(signupSchema) });

  async function onSubmit(values: SignupValues) {
    setServerError(undefined);
    try {
      const { data, error, response } = await api.POST('/api/v1/auth/signup', { body: values });
      if (data) setSent(data.message);
      else setServerError(problemMessage(error, response.status));
    } catch (e) {
      setServerError(problemMessage(e));
    }
  }

  if (sent) {
    return (
      <div className={styles.form}>
        <h1 className={styles.title}>메일을 확인해 주세요</h1>
        <p role="status" className={styles.done}>
          {sent}
        </p>
        <p className={styles.lead}>메일의 링크를 열고 가입할 때 정한 비밀번호를 한 번 더 입력하면 가입이 끝나요.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate>
      <h1 className={styles.title}>이메일로 가입</h1>
      <TextField label="닉네임" required autoComplete="nickname" error={formState.errors.nickname?.message} {...register('nickname')} />
      <TextField label="이메일" type="email" required autoComplete="email" error={formState.errors.email?.message} {...register('email')} />
      <TextField
        label="비밀번호"
        type="password"
        required
        autoComplete="new-password"
        help="10자 이상. 다른 사이트에서 쓰지 않는 비밀번호를 정해 주세요."
        error={formState.errors.password?.message}
        {...register('password')}
      />
      {serverError ? (
        <p role="alert" className={styles.error}>
          {serverError}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="lg" disabled={formState.isSubmitting}>
        가입하기
      </Button>
      <p className={styles.links}>
        <Link href="/login">이미 가입했다면 로그인하기</Link>
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
  if (done) {

        label="가입할 때 정한 비밀번호"

          {serverError}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="lg" disabled={formState.isSubmitting}>
        가입 마치기
      </Button>

}
```

`apps/web/src/components/auth/ResetForms.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Button, TextField } from '@/components/ui';
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { problemMessage } from '@/lib/api/errors';
import { type EmailValues, emailSchema, type ResetValues, resetSchema } from '@/lib/auth/schemas';
import styles from './Auth.module.css';

export function ResetRequestForm() {
  const [sent, setSent] = useState<string>();
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string>();
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState } = useForm<EmailValues>({ resolver: zodResolver(emailSchema) });

  async function onSubmit(values: EmailValues) {
    setServerError(undefined);
    try {
      const { data, error, response } = await api.POST('/api/v1/auth/password-reset', { body: values });
      if (data) setSent(data.message);
      else setServerError(problemMessage(error, response.status));
    } catch (e) {
      setServerError(problemMessage(e));
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate>
      <h1 className={styles.title}>비밀번호 찾기</h1>
      <p className={styles.lead}>가입한 이메일로 재설정 링크를 보내 드려요. 링크는 30분 동안 한 번만 쓸 수 있어요.</p>
      <TextField label="이메일" type="email" autoComplete="email" error={formState.errors.email?.message} {...register('email')} />
      {sent ? (
        <p role="status" className={styles.done}>
          {sent}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {serverError ? (
        <p role="alert" className={styles.error}>
          {serverError}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="lg" disabled={formState.isSubmitting}>
        재설정 메일 받기
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}

export function ResetConfirmForm({ token }: { token: string }) {
  const [done, setDone] = useState<string>();
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string>();
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState } = useForm<ResetValues>({ resolver: zodResolver(resetSchema) });

  async function onSubmit(values: ResetValues) {
    setServerError(undefined);
    try {
      const { data, error, response } = await api.POST('/api/v1/auth/password-reset/confirm', { body: { token, password: values.password } });
      if (data) setDone(data.message);
      else setServerError(problemMessage(error, response.status));
    } catch (e) {
      setServerError(problemMessage(e));
    }
  }

  if (done) {
    return (
      <div className={styles.form}>
        <h1 className={styles.title}>비밀번호를 바꿨어요</h1>
        <p role="status" className={styles.done}>
          {done}
        </p>
        <Button href="/login" variant="primary" size="lg">
          로그인하기
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate>
      <h1 className={styles.title}>새 비밀번호 정하기</h1>
      <TextField label="새 비밀번호" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" error={formState.errors.password?.message} {...register('password')} />
      <TextField label="새 비밀번호 확인" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" error={formState.errors.confirm?.message} {...register('confirm')} />
      {serverError ? (
        <p role="alert" className={styles.error}>
          {serverError}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="lg" disabled={formState.isSubmitting}>
        비밀번호 바꾸기
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/auth/AccountActions.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button, TextField } from '@/components/ui';
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { problemMessage } from '@/lib/api/errors';
import styles from './Auth.module.css';

export function LogoutButton() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  return (
    <Button
      variant="secondary"
      disabled={busy}
      onClick={async () => {
        setBusy(true);
        await api.POST('/api/v1/auth/logout').catch(() => undefined);
        router.replace('/');
        router.refresh();
      }}
    >
      로그아웃
    </Button>
  );
}

export function DeleteAccount() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [word, setWord] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  if (!open) {
    return (
      <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
        탈퇴하기
      </Button>
    );
  }

  async function remove() {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(undefined);
    try {
      const { response, error: problem } = await api.DELETE('/api/v1/me');
      if (response.ok) {
        router.replace('/');
        router.refresh();
        return;
      }
      setError(problemMessage(problem, response.status));
    } catch (e) {
      setError(problemMessage(e));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.danger}>
      <p className={styles.lead}>탈퇴하면 계정과 내가 만든 여행이 모두 지워지고 되돌릴 수 없어요. 구글로 가입했다면 구글 연결도 해제해요.</p>
      <TextField label="확인을 위해 탈퇴를 입력해 주세요" value={word} onChange={(e) => setWord(e.target.value)} autoComplete="off" />
      {error ? (
        <p role="alert" className={styles.error}>
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <Button variant="danger" disabled={word !== '탈퇴' || busy} onClick={remove}>
        계정과 여행 모두 지우기
      </Button>
      <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
        그만두기
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```
