import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { LoginForm } from '@/components/auth/LoginForm';

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: '로그인', robots: { index: false } };

type Props = { searchParams: Promise<{ error?: string }> };

async function WithParams({ searchParams }: Props) {
  const { error } = await searchParams;
  return <LoginForm googleError={error === 'google'} />;
}

export default function LoginPage(props: Props) {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<LoginForm googleError={false} />}>
      <WithParams {...props} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { ResetRequestForm } from '@/components/auth/ResetForms';
import { VerifyEmailForm } from '@/components/auth/VerifyEmailForm';
import { EmptyState } from '@/components/ui';

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: '이메일 인증', robots: { index: false } };

type Props = { searchParams: Promise<{ token?: string }> };

async function WithToken({ searchParams }: Props) {
  const { token } = await searchParams;
  if (!token) {
    return (
      <>
        <EmptyState illustration="empty-share" title="인증 링크가 올바르지 않아요" text="메일의 링크를 다시 열어 주세요. 링크가 만료됐다면 비밀번호 찾기로 메일을 다시 받을 수 있어요." />
        <ResetRequestForm />
      </>
    );
  }
  return <VerifyEmailForm token={token} />;
}

export default function VerifyEmailPage(props: Props) {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={null}>
      <WithToken {...props} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/(auth)/reset-password/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { ResetConfirmForm, ResetRequestForm } from '@/components/auth/ResetForms';

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: '비밀번호 재설정', robots: { index: false } };

type Props = { searchParams: Promise<{ token?: string }> };

async function WithToken({ searchParams }: Props) {
  const { token } = await searchParams;
  return token ? <ResetConfirmForm token={token} /> : <ResetRequestForm />;
}

export default function ResetPasswordPage(props: Props) {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<ResetRequestForm />}>
      <WithToken {...props} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/me/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { DeleteAccount, LogoutButton } from '@/components/auth/AccountActions';
import styles from '@/components/auth/Auth.module.css';
import { SiteHeader } from '@/components/layout/SiteHeader';
import { serverApi } from '@/lib/api/server';

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: '내 정보', robots: { index: false } };

async function Account() {
  const api = await serverApi();
  const { data: me } = await api.GET('/api/v1/me').catch(() => ({ data: undefined }));
  if (!me) redirect('/login');
  return (
    <div className={styles.form}>
      <h1 className={styles.title}>{me.nickname}님</h1>
      <p className={styles.lead}>{me.email}</p>
      <LogoutButton />
      <DeleteAccount />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function MePage() {
  return (
    <>
      <SiteHeader />
      <main id="main" className="page page-narrow">
        <Suspense fallback={null}>
          <Account />
        </Suspense>
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```

`redirect()`는 예외를 던져 동작하므로 `.catch()` 밖에서 부른다. 위 코드는 `GET`의 실패만 잡고 `redirect`는 잡지 않는다.

### Task 13: E2E, 접근성 검사, CI, 기존 화면과 비교 스크린샷

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/playwright.config.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/e2e/support.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/e2e/samples.spec.ts`, `e2e/auth.spec.ts`, `e2e/a11y.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/scripts/compare-screens.mjs`
- Modify: `apps/web/package.json` (`e2e`, `compare` 스크립트)
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (`e2e` 작업)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 앞 과제의 모든 화면. API는 `TRIPNOTE_SEED_SAMPLES=true`로 띄워야 두 샘플이 들어간다. 메일은 Mailpit(`apps/api/compose.yaml`의 `mail`, API `http://localhost:8025/api/v1`).
- Produces: `pnpm --filter @tripnote/web e2e`(API와 Mailpit이 떠 있어야 한다), `pnpm --filter @tripnote/web compare`(기존 Vite 앱과 새 앱을 같은 크기로 찍어 `apps/web/test-results/compare/`에 저장).

E2E 범위(설계 3.8 중 3번 계획에 해당하는 부분): 공개 샘플 둘러보기, 링크로 원본 일정 열기, 없는 일정, 이메일 가입부터 인증, 로그인, 내 정보, 로그아웃까지, 구글 로그인 리다이렉트, 접근성 검사. 모바일 375px와 데스크톱 1280px 두 크기로 돈다. 여행 만들기와 스톱 편집, 공유 링크, 복제는 4번과 5번 계획의 E2E에서 다룬다.

- [ ] **Step 1: Playwright 설치와 설정**

Run: `cd apps/web && pnpm add -D @playwright/test@1.64.1 @axe-core/playwright@4.14.0 && pnpm exec playwright install chromium`

`apps/web/playwright.config.ts`:

```ts
import { defineConfig } from '@playwright/test';

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: './e2e',
  fullyParallel: false,
  workers: 1,
  retries: process.env.CI ? 1 : 0,
  reporter: process.env.CI ? [['list'], ['html', { open: 'never' }]] : 'list',
  use: { baseURL: 'http://localhost:3000', trace: 'retain-on-failure', locale: 'ko-KR', timezoneId: 'Asia/Seoul' },
  projects: [
    { name: 'mobile', use: { browserName: 'chromium', viewport: { width: 375, height: 812 }, isMobile: true, hasTouch: true } },
    { name: 'desktop', use: { browserName: 'chromium', viewport: { width: 1280, height: 800 } } },
  ],
  webServer: {
    command: 'pnpm start',
    url: 'http://localhost:3000',
    reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
    timeout: 60_000,
  },
});
```

`pnpm start`는 `pnpm build`가 끝난 결과를 띄운다. E2E 전에 빌드한다.

- [ ] **Step 2: 도우미**

`apps/web/e2e/support.ts`:

```ts
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';
import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

const MAILPIT = process.env.MAILPIT_URL ?? 'http://localhost:8025';

/** Mailpit에서 받는 사람의 마지막 메일 본문을 기다려 가져온다. */
export async function latestMailText(to: string): Promise<string> {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30; attempt += 1) {
    const search = await fetch(`${MAILPIT}/api/v1/search?query=${encodeURIComponent(`to:"${to}"`)}`);
    const { messages } = (await search.json()) as { messages: { ID: string }[] };
    if (messages.length) {
      const message = await fetch(`${MAILPIT}/api/v1/message/${messages[0]!.ID}`);
      return ((await message.json()) as { Text: string }).Text;
    }
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 500));
  }
  throw new Error(`no mail for ${to}`);
}

export const tokenFrom = (text: string) => {
  const match = text.match(/token=([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/);
  if (!match) throw new Error('no token in mail');
  return match[1]!;
};

/** 심각도 serious 이상인 접근성 위반이 없어야 한다. 지도 캔버스는 MapLibre가 그리므로 뺀다. */
export async function expectAccessible(page: Page) {
  const results = await new AxeBuilder({ page }).exclude('.maplibregl-canvas').analyze();
  const serious = results.violations.filter((v) => v.impact === 'serious' || v.impact === 'critical');
  expect(serious.map((v) => `${v.id}: ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(' ')).join(', ')}`)).toEqual([]);
}

/** 지도가 기록한 중심(경도, 위도)을 읽는다. */
export async function mapCenter(page: Page): Promise<[number, number]> {
  const frame = page.locator('[data-center]');
  await expect(frame).toHaveAttribute('data-center', /,/, { timeout: 20_000 });
  const [lng, lat] = (await frame.getAttribute('data-center'))!.split(',').map(Number);
  return [lng!, lat!];
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 샘플과 일정표 E2E**

`apps/web/e2e/samples.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { mapCenter } from './support';

test('a visitor finds the public sample from home and reads it', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  const card = page.getByRole('link', { name: /다낭·호이안 가족여행/ });
  await expect(card).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: /오사카·교토/ })).toHaveCount(0);

  await card.click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/t\/danang-hoian-family-2027/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: '다낭·호이안 가족여행' })).toBeVisible();
  const days = page.getByRole('navigation', { name: '날짜 선택' }).getByRole('button');
  await expect(days).toHaveCount(5);

  await days.nth(2).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\?day=3/);
  await expect(days.nth(2)).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'date');

  const [lng, lat] = await mapCenter(page);
  expect(lat).toBeGreaterThan(10);
  expect(lat).toBeLessThan(20);
  expect(lng).toBeGreaterThan(104);
  expect(lng).toBeLessThan(112);
});

test('the Osaka Kyoto original opens by link with its four days', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/t/osaka-kyoto-family-2026?day=2');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toContainText('오사카');
  await expect(page.getByRole('navigation', { name: '날짜 선택' }).getByRole('button').nth(1)).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'date');

  const [lng, lat] = await mapCenter(page);
  expect(lat).toBeGreaterThan(33);
  expect(lat).toBeLessThan(37);
  expect(lng).toBeGreaterThan(134);
  expect(lng).toBeLessThan(137);

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '상세 일정' }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('details[data-stop-detail][open]').first()).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('navigation', { name: '여행 노트 섹션' }).getByRole('link', { name: '맛집' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#food$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '맛집 후보' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('article')).toHaveCount(10);
});

test('weather does not break a sample far in the future', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/t/danang-hoian-family-2027');
  await expect(page.getByText('아직 이 날짜의 예보가 없어요. 여행 16일 전부터 볼 수 있어요.')).toBeVisible();
});

test('an unknown or private trip shows the not found page', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/t/does-not-exist');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: '일정을 찾을 수 없어요' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.goto('/t/..%2F..%2Fapi%2Fv1%2Fme');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: '일정을 찾을 수 없어요' })).toBeVisible();
});

test('completion checks survive a reload on this device', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/t/danang-hoian-family-2027');
  const first = page.getByRole('button', { name: /^일정 완료 표시:/ }).first();
  await first.click();
  await expect(first).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');

  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /^일정 완료 표시:/ }).first()).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 인증 E2E**

`apps/web/e2e/auth.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { latestMailText, tokenFrom } from './support';

test('email signup, verification, login, account and logout', async ({ page }, info) => {
  const email = `e2e-${info.project.name}-${Date.now()}@example.com`;
  const password = 'trip-note-e2e-2027!';

  // 새 브라우저 컨텍스트라 XSRF-TOKEN 쿠키가 없다. 첫 POST가 통과해야 한다.
  await page.goto('/signup');
  await page.getByLabel(/닉네임/).fill('미나');
  await page.getByLabel(/이메일/).fill(email);
  await page.getByLabel(/비밀번호/).fill(password);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '가입하기' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('status')).toBeVisible();

  const token = tokenFrom(await latestMailText(email));
  await page.goto(`/verify-email?token=${token}`);
  await page.getByLabel('가입할 때 정한 비밀번호').fill(password);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '가입 마치기' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: '가입이 끝났어요' })).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('link', { name: '로그인하기' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('이메일').fill(email);
  await page.getByLabel('비밀번호').fill(password);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '로그인하기' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: '미나님의 여행' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('아직 만든 여행이 없어요')).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('link', { name: '내 정보' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: '미나님' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText(email)).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '로그아웃' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/');
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: '로그인하기' })).toBeVisible();

  // 로그아웃 뒤 같은 브라우저로 다시 로그인해도 CSRF 토큰이 맞아야 한다.
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.getByLabel('이메일').fill(email);
  await page.getByLabel('비밀번호').fill(password);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '로그인하기' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: '미나님의 여행' })).toBeVisible();
});

test('a wrong password is explained without saying whether the account exists', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.getByLabel('이메일').fill('nobody@example.com');
  await page.getByLabel('비밀번호').fill('not-the-password');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '로그인하기' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login$/);
});

test('the google button starts the OAuth redirect on the web origin', async ({ request }) => {
  const response = await request.get('/oauth2/authorization/google', { maxRedirects: 0 });
  expect(response.status()).toBe(302);
  const location = response.headers().location ?? '';
  expect(location).toContain('https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth');
  expect(decodeURIComponent(location)).toContain('redirect_uri=http://localhost:3000/login/oauth2/code/google');
});

test('the account page sends visitors to login', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/me');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login$/);
});
```

import { test } from '@playwright/test';
import { expectAccessible } from './support';

for (const path of ['/', '/t/osaka-kyoto-family-2026', '/t/danang-hoian-family-2027#preparation', '/login', '/signup', '/t/does-not-exist']) {

- [ ] **Step 6: 비교 스크린샷 스크립트**

`apps/web/scripts/compare-screens.mjs`:

```js
// 기존 Vite 앱(5173)과 새 앱(3000)의 같은 날을 같은 크기로 찍는다. 픽셀 일치가 아니라 정보와 순서를 사람이 비교하는 자료다.
import { mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { chromium } from '@playwright/test';

const out = new URL('../test-results/compare/', import.meta.url);
mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });

const sizes = { mobile: { width: 375, height: 812 }, desktop: { width: 1280, height: 800 } };
const browser = await chromium.launch();
for (const [name, viewport] of Object.entries(sizes)) {
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport });
  for (let day = 1; day <= 4; day += 1) {
    await page.goto(`http://localhost:5173/`);
    await page.locator(`[data-day="${day - 1}"]`).click();
    await page.screenshot({ path: new URL(`legacy-${name}-day${day}.png`, out).pathname, fullPage: true });
    await page.goto(`http://localhost:3000/t/osaka-kyoto-family-2026?day=${day}`);
    await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle');
    await page.screenshot({ path: new URL(`web-${name}-day${day}.png`, out).pathname, fullPage: true });
  }
  await page.close();
}
await browser.close();
console.log(`screens saved to ${out.pathname}`);
```

`apps/web/package.json`의 `scripts`에 `"compare": "node scripts/compare-screens.mjs"`를 더한다.

Run (세 프로세스를 띄운 채):
```bash
pnpm dev                                   # 루트, 기존 Vite 앱 5173
cd apps/api && TRIPNOTE_SEED_SAMPLES=true JAVA_HOME=/Users/hyuno/Library/Java/JavaVirtualMachines/openjdk-25.0.2/Contents/Home ./gradlew bootRun
cd apps/web && pnpm build && pnpm start
cd apps/web && pnpm compare
```
Expected: `test-results/compare/`에 16장. 날짜마다 기존 화면과 새 화면을 나란히 열어 스톱 제목과 순서, 시각, 버퍼 안내, 가이드 제목이 같은지 확인한다. 빠진 정보가 있으면 해당 과제로 돌아가 고친다. 차이 목록은 브랜치 리뷰 요청에 적는다.

- [ ] **Step 8: CI에 e2e 작업 추가**

`.github/workflows/ci.yml`의 `jobs:` 아래에 더한다(액션 SHA는 기존 작업과 같다):

```yaml
  e2e:
    needs: [api, web]
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 30
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5
      - uses: actions/setup-java@b6effb05e454b25005698d916606bdc6ffcbf961 # v5
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: 25
      - uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@0723195856401067f7a2779048b490ace7a47d7c # v5
      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@b906affcce14559ad1aafd4ab0e942779e9f58b1 # v4
        with:
          version: 10
      - uses: actions/setup-node@a0853c24544627f65ddf259abe73b1d18a591444 # v5
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: pnpm
      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
      - name: start database, valkey and mailpit
        working-directory: apps/api
        run: docker compose up -d --wait
      - name: start api with samples
        working-directory: apps/api
        run: |
          ./gradlew bootJar
          JAR=$(find build/libs -name '*.jar' ! -name '*-plain.jar' | head -1)
          TRIPNOTE_SEED_SAMPLES=true nohup java -jar "$JAR" > api.log 2>&1 &
          timeout 120 bash -c 'until curl -sf http://localhost:8080/actuator/health/readiness; do sleep 2; done'
      - name: e2e
        working-directory: apps/web
        run: |
          pnpm exec playwright install --with-deps chromium
          pnpm build
          pnpm e2e
      - name: api log on failure
        if: failure()
        run: tail -n 200 apps/api/api.log
```
